Agent Action ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

Summary: Create gameEngine with Connect 4 rules, minimax search and transplant 
preview

Reasoning:
**Okay, let's get down to brass tacks on this game engine.** I'm going to 
structure `/app/gameEngine.js` meticulously. My thinking is to break this down 
into discrete, testable units, starting with the fundamentals:

1.  **Board Representation and Coordinate Systems:** I'll define `ROWS`, `COLS`,
`NUM_CELLS`, and lay out how I'll convert between row/column and cell indices. 
`indexToRow`, `indexToCol`, and a clean `createEmptyBoard()` function are 
essential building blocks.

2.  **Move Validation and Gravity:** The core functions here will be about 
checking move legality. `getLowestEmptyRow()`, `isColumnFull()`, and 
`getLegalColumns()` will be the backbone, ensuring no moves are made where they 
don't belong.

3.  **Applying a Move:**  The `applyMove()` function needs to be pure and 
immutable. It'll take the board state, column, and color and return a *new* 
board reflecting the move. Error handling is paramount, especially when handling
full columns, and I'll return the new state along with move details.

4.  **Win and Draw Detection:** This is critical. `checkWin()` needs to be 
efficient, both checking for a win and providing *precisely* the winning cells. 
The `evaluateBoardState()` function will orchestrate win/draw detection, taking 
an *optional* `lastMove` for performance. If no last move, this function will go
through the entire board.

5.  **State Derivation:**  `deriveStateFromMoves()` will be used to reconstruct 
a game state from a series of moves. It starts with an empty board and then 
incrementally applies the moves, updating the board, current player, and the 
game status. This is to make sure we're starting from a correct state, and also 
can re-create a board from a given set of moves.

6.  **Board Diffing:**  `diffBoards()` will compare two board states and 
identify the cells that have changed. It's not strictly core to the game, but 
essential for visual diffing and analysis.

7.  **Common Move Prefix:**  `findCommonPrefixLength()` will be utilized for 
quickly identifying the longest identical sequence of moves between two game 
states. This will be very useful.

8.  **Tactical Report (Minimax):**  This is where the real intelligence comes 
in, and I want a bounded-depth Minimax. `computeTacticalReport()` needs to be 
designed very thoroughly. I will need to consider the depth parameter, and 
ensure the rules for horizon evaluation and minimax tree traversal are 
implemented correctly. The structure of the proof nodes (`id`, `columnPath`, 
`lastMove`, etc.) and the precise Minimax evaluation logic must be rigorously 
defined. The outcomes ("forced\_win", "forced\_loss", "forced\_draw", 
"not\_established") and distances are key. I must ensure the tree logic is 
implemented correctly for both the player and the opponent's perspective. The 
function must return a full proof tree.

9.  **Transplant Preview and Tree Algorithm:** This is about merging analysis 
from different game states. The `simulateTransplant()` function is the heart of 
it.  I'll start with the root source, recursively build a "preview" of the 
transplant operation with the simulation logic. It checks for move legality 
(column fullness, game end), and determines if a destination node has been 
reused. The preview nodes will record the simulated board state, move details, 
status, and whether a previous destination node was reused. It needs to handle 
potentially invalid states resulting from the simulation and will return errors 
as needed. The first illegal move must be discovered.

I will write comprehensive unit tests for all functions and handle all edge 
cases to ensure correctness.  Then `/app/gameEngine.js` will be 100% reliable.




Action: FileEditorAction

Arguments:
  command: "create"
  path: "/app/gameEngine.js"
  file_text: 
    const ROWS = 6;
    const COLS = 7;
    const TOTAL_CELLS = 42;
    
    function createEmptyBoard() {
      return Array(TOTAL_CELLS).fill("");
    }
    
    function cellIndex(row, col) {
      // row: 1..6, col: 1..7
      return (row - 1) * COLS + (col - 1);
    }
    
    function indexToRow(idx) {
      return Math.floor(idx / COLS) + 1;
    }
    
    function indexToCol(idx) {
      return (idx % COLS) + 1;
    }
    
    function getLowestEmptyRow(board, col) {
      if (col < 1 || col > COLS) return null;
      for (let r = ROWS; r >= 1; r--) {
        const idx = cellIndex(r, col);
        if (!board[idx]) {
          return r;
        }
      }
      return null;
    }
    
    function isColumnFull(board, col) {
      return getLowestEmptyRow(board, col) === null;
    }
    
    function getLegalColumns(board) {
      const cols = [];
      for (let c = 1; c <= COLS; c++) {
        if (!isColumnFull(board, c)) {
          cols.push(c);
        }
      }
      return cols;
    }
    
    function isBoardFull(board) {
      return board.every(cell => cell === 'Red' || cell === 'Yellow');
    }
    
    function checkWin(board, lastRow, lastCol, color) {
      if (!color) return { won: false, winningCells: [] };
    
      const directions = [
        { dr: 0, dc: 1 },  // Horizontal
        { dr: 1, dc: 0 },  // Vertical
        { dr: 1, dc: 1 },  // Diagonal \
        { dr: -1, dc: 1 }  // Diagonal /
      ];
    
      for (const { dr, dc } of directions) {
        // Collect consecutive cells in this direction passing through (lastRow,
lastCol)
        const line = [{ r: lastRow, c: lastCol, idx: cellIndex(lastRow, lastCol)
}];
    
        // Forward
        let step = 1;
        while (true) {
          const r = lastRow + dr * step;
          const c = lastCol + dc * step;
          if (r < 1 || r > ROWS || c < 1 || c > COLS) break;
          const idx = cellIndex(r, c);
          if (board[idx] === color) {
            line.push({ r, c, idx });
            step++;
          } else {
            break;
          }
        }
    
        // Backward
        step = 1;
        while (true) {
          const r = lastRow - dr * step;
          const c = lastCol - dc * step;
          if (r < 1 || r > ROWS || c < 1 || c > COLS) break;
          const idx = cellIndex(r, c);
          if (board[idx] === color) {
            line.unshift({ r, c, idx });
            step++;
          } else {
            break;
          }
        }
    
        if (line.length >= 4) {
          // Find the 4 consecutive cells that include the last played piece
          const lastPieceIndexInLine = line.findIndex(pt => pt.r === lastRow && 
pt.c === lastCol);
          // Pick 4 contiguous cells containing lastPieceIndexInLine
          let start = Math.max(0, lastPieceIndexInLine - 3);
          let end = Math.min(line.length - 4, lastPieceIndexInLine);
          const chosenStart = Math.min(start, end);
          const winning4 = line.slice(chosenStart, chosenStart + 4).map(pt => 
pt.idx);
          return { won: true, winningCells: winning4 };
        }
      }
    
      return { won: false, winningCells: [] };
    }
    
    function checkBoardAnyWin(board) {
      // Check horizontal
      for (let r = 1; r <= ROWS; r++) {
        for (let c = 1; c <= COLS - 3; c++) {
          const idx1 = cellIndex(r, c);
          const color = board[idx1];
          if (color && color === board[cellIndex(r, c+1)] && color === 
board[cellIndex(r, c+2)] && color === board[cellIndex(r, c+3)]) {
            return { won: true, winner: color, winningCells: [idx1, cellIndex(r,
c+1), cellIndex(r, c+2), cellIndex(r, c+3)] };
          }
        }
      }
    
      // Check vertical
      for (let c = 1; c <= COLS; c++) {
        for (let r = 1; r <= ROWS - 3; r++) {
          const idx1 = cellIndex(r, c);
          const color = board[idx1];
          if (color && color === board[cellIndex(r+1, c)] && color === 
board[cellIndex(r+2, c)] && color === board[cellIndex(r+3, c)]) {
            return { won: true, winner: color, winningCells: [idx1, 
cellIndex(r+1, c), cellIndex(r+2, c), cellIndex(r+3, c)] };
          }
        }
      }
    
      // Diagonal Down-Right \
      for (let r = 1; r <= ROWS - 3; r++) {
        for (let c = 1; c <= COLS - 3; c++) {
          const idx1 = cellIndex(r, c);
          const color = board[idx1];
          if (color && color === board[cellIndex(r+1, c+1)] && color === 
board[cellIndex(r+2, c+2)] && color === board[cellIndex(r+3, c+3)]) {
            return { won: true, winner: color, winningCells: [idx1, 
cellIndex(r+1, c+1), cellIndex(r+2, c+2), cellIndex(r+3, c+3)] };
          }
        }
      }
    
      // Diagonal Up-Right /
      for (let r = 4; r <= ROWS; r++) {
        for (let c = 1; c <= COLS - 3; c++) {
          const idx1 = cellIndex(r, c);
          const color = board[idx1];
          if (color && color === board[cellIndex(r-1, c+1)] && color === 
board[cellIndex(r-2, c+2)] && color === board[cellIndex(r-3, c+3)]) {
            return { won: true, winner: color, winningCells: [idx1, 
cellIndex(r-1, c+1), cellIndex(r-2, c+2), cellIndex(r-3, c+3)] };
          }
        }
      }
    
      return { won: false, winner: null, winningCells: [] };
    }
    
    function evaluateBoardState(board, lastMove) {
      if (lastMove && lastMove.row && lastMove.column && lastMove.color) {
        const winResult = checkWin(board, lastMove.row, lastMove.column, 
lastMove.color);
        if (winResult.won) {
          return {
            status: `${lastMove.color} wins`,
            winner: lastMove.color,
            winningCells: winResult.winningCells
          };
        }
      } else {
        const fullWin = checkBoardAnyWin(board);
        if (fullWin.won) {
          return {
            status: `${fullWin.winner} wins`,
            winner: fullWin.winner,
            winningCells: fullWin.winningCells
          };
        }
      }
    
      if (isBoardFull(board)) {
        return {
          status: 'Draw',
          winner: null,
          winningCells: []
        };
      }
    
      return {
        status: 'active',
        winner: null,
        winningCells: []
      };
    }
    
    function applyMoveToBoard(board, col, color) {
      const row = getLowestEmptyRow(board, col);
      if (row === null) {
        throw new Error(`Column ${col} is full`);
      }
      const idx = cellIndex(row, col);
      const newBoard = [...board];
      newBoard[idx] = color;
      const lastMove = { row, column: col, index: idx, color };
      const evalState = evaluateBoardState(newBoard, lastMove);
    
      return {
        board: newBoard,
        lastMove,
        status: evalState.status,
        winner: evalState.winner,
        winningCells: evalState.winningCells
      };
    }
    
    function deriveStateFromMoves(moves) {
      let board = createEmptyBoard();
      let currentPlayer = 'Red';
      let lastMove = null;
    
      for (let i = 0; i < moves.length; i++) {
        const m = moves[i];
        const color = m.color || currentPlayer;
        const col = m.column;
        const res = applyMoveToBoard(board, col, color);
        board = res.board;
        lastMove = res.lastMove;
        currentPlayer = color === 'Red' ? 'Yellow' : 'Red';
      }
    
      const evalState = evaluateBoardState(board, lastMove);
      return {
        board,
        currentPlayer,
        status: evalState.status,
        winner: evalState.winner,
        winningCells: evalState.winningCells
      };
    }
    
    function diffBoards(boardA, boardB) {
      const differences = [];
      for (let i = 0; i < TOTAL_CELLS; i++) {
        const occA = boardA[i] || 'empty';
        const occB = boardB[i] || 'empty';
        if (occA !== occB) {
          differences.push({
            index: i,
            row: indexToRow(i),
            column: indexToCol(i),
            occupantA: occA,
            occupantB: occB
          });
        }
      }
      return differences;
    }
    
    function findCommonPrefixLength(movesA, movesB) {
      let count = 0;
      const minLen = Math.min(movesA.length, movesB.length);
      for (let i = 0; i < minLen; i++) {
        if (movesA[i].column === movesB[i].column && movesA[i].color === 
movesB[i].color) {
          count++;
        } else {
          break;
        }
      }
      return count;
    }
    
    // Bounded Tactical Search
    function computeTacticalReport(rootBoard, rootPlayerToMove, maxDepth, 
existingStatus, existingWinningCells) {
      // Check if root is already terminal
      const initialEval = evaluateBoardState(rootBoard);
      if (initialEval.status !== 'active') {
        return {
          isTerminal: true,
          rootOutcome: initialEval.status,
          playerToMove: rootPlayerToMove,
          depth: maxDepth,
          columns: [],
          tree: {
            id: 'root',
            columnPath: [],
            lastMove: null,
            board: rootBoard,
            player: rootPlayerToMove,
            perspective: rootPlayerToMove,
            status: initialEval.status,
            winningCells: initialEval.winningCells,
            isTerminal: true,
            isHorizon: false,
            outcome: initialEval.status === 'Draw' ? 'forced_draw' : 
(initialEval.winner === rootPlayerToMove ? 'forced_win' : 'forced_loss'),
            distance: 0,
            children: []
          }
        };
      }
    
      // Recursive minimax proof tree builder
      function buildProofNode(board, player, remainingDepth, path, lastMove) {
        const pathKey = path.length === 0 ? 'root' : `col-${path.join('-')}`;
        const evalState = evaluateBoardState(board, lastMove);
    
        // If terminal
        if (evalState.status !== 'active') {
          const isWin = evalState.winner !== null;
          const isDraw = evalState.status === 'Draw';
          // From player's perspective (the player whose turn it would be):
          // The previous player just won or drew
          const outcome = isDraw ? 'forced_draw' : 'forced_loss';
          return {
            id: pathKey,
            columnPath: path,
            lastMove,
            board,
            player,
            perspective: player,
            status: evalState.status,
            winningCells: evalState.winningCells,
            isTerminal: true,
            isHorizon: false,
            outcome,
            distance: 0,
            children: []
          };
        }
    
        // If depth 0 reached and nonterminal
        if (remainingDepth === 0) {
          return {
            id: pathKey,
            columnPath: path,
            lastMove,
            board,
            player,
            perspective: player,
            status: 'active',
            winningCells: [],
            isTerminal: false,
            isHorizon: true,
            outcome: 'not_established',
            distance: null,
            children: []
          };
        }
    
        // Nonterminal with remaining depth > 0: examine legal children in 
ascending column order
        const legalCols = getLegalColumns(board);
        const opponent = player === 'Red' ? 'Yellow' : 'Red';
        const children = [];
    
        for (const col of legalCols) {
          const moveRes = applyMoveToBoard(board, col, player);
          const childNode = buildProofNode(
            moveRes.board,
            opponent,
            remainingDepth - 1,
            [...path, col],
            moveRes.lastMove
          );
          children.push({
            col,
            childNode
          });
        }
    
        // Evaluate current node outcome from `player` perspective based on 
children
        // Each child was evaluated from `opponent` perspective.
        // Opponent forced_loss -> Player forced_win
        // Opponent forced_win -> Player forced_loss
        // Opponent forced_draw -> Player forced_draw
        // Opponent not_established -> Player not_established
    
        const childResultsForPlayer = children.map(({ col, childNode }) => {
          let outcomeForPlayer;
          let distForPlayer = null;
    
          if (childNode.outcome === 'forced_loss') {
            outcomeForPlayer = 'forced_win';
            distForPlayer = (childNode.distance || 0) + 1;
          } else if (childNode.outcome === 'forced_win') {
            outcomeForPlayer = 'forced_loss';
            distForPlayer = (childNode.distance || 0) + 1;
          } else if (childNode.outcome === 'forced_draw') {
            outcomeForPlayer = 'forced_draw';
            distForPlayer = null;
          } else {
            outcomeForPlayer = 'not_established';
            distForPlayer = null;
          }
    
          return {
            col,
            outcome: outcomeForPlayer,
            distance: distForPlayer,
            childNode
          };
        });
    
        let overallOutcome = 'not_established';
        let overallDistance = null;
    
        const winChildren = childResultsForPlayer.filter(c => c.outcome === 
'forced_win');
        const lossChildren = childResultsForPlayer.filter(c => c.outcome === 
'forced_loss');
        const drawChildren = childResultsForPlayer.filter(c => c.outcome === 
'forced_draw');
        const unknownChildren = childResultsForPlayer.filter(c => c.outcome === 
'not_established');
    
        if (winChildren.length > 0) {
          // Shortest winning distance
          overallOutcome = 'forced_win';
          overallDistance = Math.min(...winChildren.map(c => c.distance));
        } else if (lossChildren.length === childResultsForPlayer.length && 
childResultsForPlayer.length > 0) {
          // All children are proven losses -> longest distance (best 
resistance)
          overallOutcome = 'forced_loss';
          overallDistance = Math.max(...lossChildren.map(c => c.distance));
        } else if (winChildren.length === 0 && unknownChildren.length === 0 && 
drawChildren.length > 0) {
          // No winning, no unknown, at least one draw
          overallOutcome = 'forced_draw';
          overallDistance = null;
        } else {
          overallOutcome = 'not_established';
          overallDistance = null;
        }
    
        return {
          id: pathKey,
          columnPath: path,
          lastMove,
          board,
          player,
          perspective: player,
          status: 'active',
          winningCells: [],
          isTerminal: false,
          isHorizon: false,
          outcome: overallOutcome,
          distance: overallDistance,
          children: children.map(c => c.childNode),
          _columnEvaluations: childResultsForPlayer
        };
      }
    
      const rootProof = buildProofNode(rootBoard, rootPlayerToMove, maxDepth, 
[], null);
    
      const columnSummaries = (rootProof._columnEvaluations || []).map(evalItem 
=> {
        let description;
        if (evalItem.outcome === 'forced_win') {
          description = `Forced win in ${evalItem.distance} ${evalItem.distance 
=== 1 ? 'ply' : 'plies'}`;
        } else if (evalItem.outcome === 'forced_loss') {
          description = `Forced loss in ${evalItem.distance} ${evalItem.distance
=== 1 ? 'ply' : 'plies'}`;
        } else if (evalItem.outcome === 'forced_draw') {
          description = 'Forced draw';
        } else {
          description = `Not established within search depth (${maxDepth} 
${maxDepth === 1 ? 'ply' : 'plies'})`;
        }
    
        return {
          column: evalItem.col,
          outcome: evalItem.outcome,
          distance: evalItem.distance,
          description
        };
      });
    
      // Clean up private helper properties before returning
      function cleanNode(n) {
        delete n._columnEvaluations;
        if (n.children) {
          n.children.forEach(cleanNode);
        }
      }
      cleanNode(rootProof);
    
      return {
        isTerminal: false,
        rootOutcome: null,
        playerToMove: rootPlayerToMove,
        depth: maxDepth,
        columns: columnSummaries,
        tree: rootProof
      };
    }
    
    // Branch Preview and Transplant Logic
    function previewTransplant(sourceNode, sourceAllNodes, destNode, 
destAllNodes) {
      // Build lookup maps
      const sourceNodeMap = new Map();
      for (const n of sourceAllNodes) {
        sourceNodeMap.set(n.id, n);
      }
    
      const sourceChildrenMap = new Map();
      for (const n of sourceAllNodes) {
        if (n.parent_id) {
          if (!sourceChildrenMap.has(n.parent_id)) {
            sourceChildrenMap.set(n.parent_id, []);
          }
          sourceChildrenMap.get(n.parent_id).push(n);
        }
      }
    
      const destChildrenMap = new Map();
      for (const n of destAllNodes) {
        if (n.parent_id) {
          if (!destChildrenMap.has(n.parent_id)) {
            destChildrenMap.set(n.parent_id, []);
          }
          destChildrenMap.get(n.parent_id).push(n);
        }
      }
    
      if (!sourceNode.parent_id || sourceNode.incoming_column === null) {
        return {
          valid: false,
          error: {
            path: [],
            reason: 'Cannot transplant from the root position. Choose a branch 
node.'
          }
        };
      }
    
      const previewNodes = [];
      let newCount = 0;
      let reusedCount = 0;
    
      // Recursive DFS traversal
      function simulateNode(currentSrcNode, parentDestBoard, parentDestTurn, 
parentDestId, relativePath) {
        const col = currentSrcNode.incoming_column;
        const currentPath = [...relativePath, col];
    
        // Check if parentDestBoard is terminal
        const parentEval = evaluateBoardState(parentDestBoard);
        if (parentEval.status !== 'active') {
          return {
            valid: false,
            error: {
              path: currentPath,
              reason: `Position is terminal (${parentEval.status})`
            }
          };
        }
    
        // Check if column is full
        if (isColumnFull(parentDestBoard, col)) {
          return {
            valid: false,
            error: {
              path: currentPath,
              reason: `Column ${col} is full`
            }
          };
        }
    
        // Apply move
        const moveRes = applyMoveToBoard(parentDestBoard, col, parentDestTurn);
        const nextTurn = parentDestTurn === 'Red' ? 'Yellow' : 'Red';
    
        // Check if parentDestId has existing child with incoming_column === col
        let existingChild = null;
        if (parentDestId && destChildrenMap.has(parentDestId)) {
          existingChild = destChildrenMap.get(parentDestId).find(c => 
c.incoming_column === col);
        }
    
        let isReused = false;
        let reusedDestNodeId = null;
    
        if (existingChild) {
          isReused = true;
          reusedDestNodeId = existingChild.id;
          reusedCount++;
        } else {
          newCount++;
        }
    
        previewNodes.push({
          sourceNodeId: currentSrcNode.id,
          relativePath: currentPath,
          incomingColumn: col,
          color: parentDestTurn,
          landingRow: moveRes.lastMove.row,
          landingCol: moveRes.lastMove.column,
          landingIndex: moveRes.lastMove.index,
          board: moveRes.board,
          turn: nextTurn,
          status: moveRes.status,
          winningCells: moveRes.winningCells,
          reused: isReused,
          reusedDestNodeId: reusedDestNodeId,
          parentSourceId: currentSrcNode.parent_id
        });
    
        // Inspect children of currentSrcNode sorted by ascending 
incoming_column
        const srcChildren = (sourceChildrenMap.get(currentSrcNode.id) || [])
          .slice()
          .sort((a, b) => a.incoming_column - b.incoming_column);
    
        for (const child of srcChildren) {
          const childRes = simulateNode(
            child,
            moveRes.board,
            nextTurn,
            reusedDestNodeId,
            currentPath
          );
          if (!childRes.valid) {
            return childRes;
          }
        }
    
        return { valid: true };
      }
    
      const parseBoard = (b) => (typeof b === 'string' ? JSON.parse(b) : b);
      const destBoard = parseBoard(destNode.board);
      const destTurn = destNode.turn;
    
      const simResult = simulateNode(sourceNode, destBoard, destTurn, 
destNode.id, []);
      if (!simResult.valid) {
        return {
          valid: false,
          error: simResult.error
        };
      }
    
      return {
        valid: true,
        previewNodes,
        newCount,
        reusedCount
      };
    }
    
    module.exports = {
      ROWS,
      COLS,
      TOTAL_CELLS,

+22|function getLowestEmptyRow(board, col) {
+23|  if (col < 1 || col > COLS) return null;
+24|  for (let r = ROWS; r >= 1; r--) {
+25|    const idx = cellIndex(r, col);
+26|    if (!board[idx]) {
+27|      return r;
+28|    }
+29|  }
+30|  return null;

+47|function isBoardFull(board) {
+48|  return board.every(cell => cell === 'Red' || cell === 'Yellow');
+49|}
+50|
+51|function checkWin(board, lastRow, lastCol, color) {
+52|  if (!color) return { won: false, winningCells: [] };
+53|
+54|  const directions = [
+55|    { dr: 0, dc: 1 },  // Horizontal
+56|    { dr: 1, dc: 0 },  // Vertical
+57|    { dr: 1, dc: 1 },  // Diagonal \
+58|    { dr: -1, dc: 1 }  // Diagonal /
+59|  ];
+60|
+61|  for (const { dr, dc } of directions) {
+62|    // Collect consecutive cells in this direction passing through (lastRow,
lastCol)
+63|    const line = [{ r: lastRow, c: lastCol, idx: cellIndex(lastRow, lastCol)
}];
+64|
+65|    // Forward
+66|    let step = 1;
+67|    while (true) {
+68|      const r = lastRow + dr * step;
+69|      const c = lastCol + dc * step;
+70|      if (r < 1 || r > ROWS || c < 1 || c > COLS) break;
+71|      const idx = cellIndex(r, c);
+72|      if (board[idx] === color) {
+73|        line.push({ r, c, idx });
+74|        step++;
+75|      } else {
+76|        break;
+77|      }
+78|    }
+79|
+80|    // Backward
+81|    step = 1;
+82|    while (true) {
+83|      const r = lastRow - dr * step;
+84|      const c = lastCol - dc * step;
+85|      if (r < 1 || r > ROWS || c < 1 || c > COLS) break;
+86|      const idx = cellIndex(r, c);
+87|      if (board[idx] === color) {
+88|        line.unshift({ r, c, idx });
+89|        step++;
+90|      } else {
+91|        break;
+92|      }
+93|    }
+94|
+95|    if (line.length >= 4) {
+96|      // Find the 4 consecutive cells that include the last played piece
+97|      const lastPieceIndexInLine = line.findIndex(pt => pt.r === lastRow && 
pt.c === lastCol);
+98|      // Pick 4 contiguous cells containing lastPieceIndexInLine
+99|      let start = Math.max(0, lastPieceIndexInLine - 3);
+100|      let end = Math.min(line.length - 4, lastPieceIndexInLine);
+101|      const chosenStart = Math.min(start, end);
+102|      const winning4 = line.slice(chosenStart, chosenStart + 4).map(pt => 
pt.idx);
+103|      return { won: true, winningCells: winning4 };
+104|    }
+105|  }
+106|
+107|  return { won: false, winningCells: [] };
+108|}
+109|
+110|function checkBoardAnyWin(board) {
+111|  // Check horizontal
+112|  for (let r = 1; r <= ROWS; r++) {
+113|    for (let c = 1; c <= COLS - 3; c++) {
+114|      const idx1 = cellIndex(r, c);
+115|      const color = board[idx1];
+116|      if (color && color === board[cellIndex(r, c+1)] && color === 
board[cellIndex(r, c+2)] && color === board[cellIndex(r, c+3)]) {
+117|        return { won: true, winner: color, winningCells: [idx1, 
cellIndex(r, c+1), cellIndex(r, c+2), cellIndex(r, c+3)] };
+118|      }
+119|    }
+120|  }
+121|
+122|  // Check vertical
+123|  for (let c = 1; c <= COLS; c++) {
+124|    for (let r = 1; r <= ROWS - 3; r++) {
+125|      const idx1 = cellIndex(r, c);
+126|      const color = board[idx1];
+127|      if (color && color === board[cellIndex(r+1, c)] && color === 
board[cellIndex(r+2, c)] && color === board[cellIndex(r+3, c)]) {
+128|        return { won: true, winner: color, winningCells: [idx1, 
cellIndex(r+1, c), cellIndex(r+2, c), cellIndex(r+3, c)] };
+129|      }
+130|    }
+131|  }
+132|
+133|  // Diagonal Down-Right \
+134|  for (let r = 1; r <= ROWS - 3; r++) {
+135|    for (let c = 1; c <= COLS - 3; c++) {
+136|      const idx1 = cellIndex(r, c);
+137|      const color = board[idx1];
+138|      if (color && color === board[cellIndex(r+1, c+1)] && color === 
board[cellIndex(r+2, c+2)] && color === board[cellIndex(r+3, c+3)]) {
+139|        return { won: true, winner: color, winningCells: [idx1, 
cellIndex(r+1, c+1), cellIndex(r+2, c+2), cellIndex(r+3, c+3)] };
+140|      }
+141|    }
+142|  }
+143|
+144|  // Diagonal Up-Right /
+145|  for (let r = 4; r <= ROWS; r++) {
+146|    for (let c = 1; c <= COLS - 3; c++) {
+147|      const idx1 = cellIndex(r, c);
+148|      const color = board[idx1];
+149|      if (color && color === board[cellIndex(r-1, c+1)] && color === 
board[cellIndex(r-2, c+2)] && color === board[cellIndex(r-3, c+3)]) {
+150|        return { won: true, winner: color, winningCells: [idx1, 
cellIndex(r-1, c+1), cellIndex(r-2, c+2), cellIndex(r-3, c+3)] };
+151|      }
+152|    }
+153|  }
+154|
+155|  return { won: false, winner: null, winningCells: [] };
+156|}
+157|
+158|function evaluateBoardState(board, lastMove) {
+159|  if (lastMove && lastMove.row && lastMove.column && lastMove.color) {
+160|    const winResult = checkWin(board, lastMove.row, lastMove.column, 
lastMove.color);
+161|    if (winResult.won) {
+162|      return {
+163|        status: `${lastMove.color} wins`,
+164|        winner: lastMove.color,
+165|        winningCells: winResult.winningCells
+166|      };
+167|    }
+168|  } else {
+169|    const fullWin = checkBoardAnyWin(board);
+170|    if (fullWin.won) {
+171|      return {
+172|        status: `${fullWin.winner} wins`,
+173|        winner: fullWin.winner,
+174|        winningCells: fullWin.winningCells
+175|      };
+176|    }
+177|  }
+178|
+179|  if (isBoardFull(board)) {
+180|    return {
+181|      status: 'Draw',
+182|      winner: null,
+183|      winningCells: []
+184|    };
+185|  }
+186|
+187|  return {
+188|    status: 'active',
+189|    winner: null,
+190|    winningCells: []
+191|  };
+192|}
+193|
+194|function applyMoveToBoard(board, col, color) {
+195|  const row = getLowestEmptyRow(board, col);
+196|  if (row === null) {
+197|    throw new Error(`Column ${col} is full`);
+198|  }
+199|  const idx = cellIndex(row, col);
+200|  const newBoard = [...board];
+201|  newBoard[idx] = color;
+202|  const lastMove = { row, column: col, index: idx, color };
+203|  const evalState = evaluateBoardState(newBoard, lastMove);
+204|
+205|  return {
+206|    board: newBoard,
+207|    lastMove,
+208|    status: evalState.status,
+209|    winner: evalState.winner,
+210|    winningCells: evalState.winningCells
+211|  };
+212|}
+213|
+214|function deriveStateFromMoves(moves) {
+215|  let board = createEmptyBoard();
+216|  let currentPlayer = 'Red';
+217|  let lastMove = null;
+218|
+219|  for (let i = 0; i < moves.length; i++) {
+220|    const m = moves[i];
+221|    const color = m.color || currentPlayer;
+222|    const col = m.column;
+223|    const res = applyMoveToBoard(board, col, color);
+224|    board = res.board;
+225|    lastMove = res.lastMove;
+226|    currentPlayer = color === 'Red' ? 'Yellow' : 'Red';
+227|  }
+228|
+229|  const evalState = evaluateBoardState(board, lastMove);
+230|  return {
+231|    board,
+232|    currentPlayer,
+233|    status: evalState.status,
+234|    winner: evalState.winner,
+235|    winningCells: evalState.winningCells
+236|  };
+237|}
+238|
+239|function diffBoards(boardA, boardB) {
+240|  const differences = [];
+241|  for (let i = 0; i < TOTAL_CELLS; i++) {
+242|    const occA = boardA[i] || 'empty';
+243|    const occB = boardB[i] || 'empty';
+244|    if (occA !== occB) {
+245|      differences.push({
+246|        index: i,
+247|        row: indexToRow(i),
+248|        column: indexToCol(i),
+249|        occupantA: occA,
+250|        occupantB: occB
+251|      });
+252|    }
+253|  }
+254|  return differences;
+255|}
+256|
+257|function findCommonPrefixLength(movesA, movesB) {
+258|  let count = 0;
+259|  const minLen = Math.min(movesA.length, movesB.length);
+260|  for (let i = 0; i < minLen; i++) {
+261|    if (movesA[i].column === movesB[i].column && movesA[i].color === 
movesB[i].color) {
+262|      count++;
+263|    } else {
+264|      break;
+265|    }
+266|  }
+267|  return count;
+268|}

+270|// Bounded Tactical Search
+271|function computeTacticalReport(rootBoard, rootPlayerToMove, maxDepth, 
existingStatus, existingWinningCells) {
+272|  // Check if root is already terminal
+273|  const initialEval = evaluateBoardState(rootBoard);
+274|  if (initialEval.status !== 'active') {
+275|    return {
+276|      isTerminal: true,
+277|      rootOutcome: initialEval.status,
+278|      playerToMove: rootPlayerToMove,
+279|      depth: maxDepth,
+280|      columns: [],
+281|      tree: {
+282|        id: 'root',
+283|        columnPath: [],
+284|        lastMove: null,
+285|        board: rootBoard,
+286|        player: rootPlayerToMove,
+287|        perspective: rootPlayerToMove,
+288|        status: initialEval.status,
+289|        winningCells: initialEval.winningCells,
+290|        isTerminal: true,
+291|        isHorizon: false,
+292|        outcome: initialEval.status === 'Draw' ? 'forced_draw' : 
(initialEval.winner === rootPlayerToMove ? 'forced_win' : 'forced_loss'),
+293|        distance: 0,
+294|        children: []
+295|      }
+296|    };
+297|  }
+298|
+299|  // Recursive minimax proof tree builder
+300|  function buildProofNode(board, player, remainingDepth, path, lastMove) {
+301|    const pathKey = path.length === 0 ? 'root' : `col-${path.join('-')}`;
+302|    const evalState = evaluateBoardState(board, lastMove);
+303|
+304|    // If terminal
+305|    if (evalState.status !== 'active') {
+306|      const isWin = evalState.winner !== null;
+307|      const isDraw = evalState.status === 'Draw';
+308|      // From player's perspective (the player whose turn it would be):
+309|      // The previous player just won or drew
+310|      const outcome = isDraw ? 'forced_draw' : 'forced_loss';
+311|      return {
+312|        id: pathKey,
+313|        columnPath: path,
+314|        lastMove,
+315|        board,
+316|        player,
+317|        perspective: player,
+318|        status: evalState.status,
+319|        winningCells: evalState.winningCells,
+320|        isTerminal: true,
+321|        isHorizon: false,
+322|        outcome,
+323|        distance: 0,
+324|        children: []
+325|      };
+326|    }
+327|
+328|    // If depth 0 reached and nonterminal
+329|    if (remainingDepth === 0) {
+330|      return {
+331|        id: pathKey,
+332|        columnPath: path,
+333|        lastMove,
+334|        board,
+335|        player,
+336|        perspective: player,
+337|        status: 'active',
+338|        winningCells: [],
+339|        isTerminal: false,
+340|        isHorizon: true,
+341|        outcome: 'not_established',
+342|        distance: null,
+343|        children: []
+344|      };
+345|    }
+346|
+347|    // Nonterminal with remaining depth > 0: examine legal children in 
ascending column order
+348|    const legalCols = getLegalColumns(board);
+349|    const opponent = player === 'Red' ? 'Yellow' : 'Red';
+350|    const children = [];
+351|
+352|    for (const col of legalCols) {
+353|      const moveRes = applyMoveToBoard(board, col, player);
+354|      const childNode = buildProofNode(
+355|        moveRes.board,
+356|        opponent,
+357|        remainingDepth - 1,
+358|        [...path, col],
+359|        moveRes.lastMove
+360|      );
+361|      children.push({
+362|        col,
+363|        childNode
+364|      });
+365|    }
+366|
+367|    // Evaluate current node outcome from `player` perspective based on 
children
+368|    // Each child was evaluated from `opponent` perspective.
+369|    // Opponent forced_loss -> Player forced_win
+370|    // Opponent forced_win -> Player forced_loss
+371|    // Opponent forced_draw -> Player forced_draw
+372|    // Opponent not_established -> Player not_established
+373|
+374|    const childResultsForPlayer = children.map(({ col, childNode }) => {
+375|      let outcomeForPlayer;
+376|      let distForPlayer = null;
+377|
+378|      if (childNode.outcome === 'forced_loss') {
+379|        outcomeForPlayer = 'forced_win';
+380|        distForPlayer = (childNode.distance || 0) + 1;
+381|      } else if (childNode.outcome === 'forced_win') {
+382|        outcomeForPlayer = 'forced_loss';
+383|        distForPlayer = (childNode.distance || 0) + 1;
+384|      } else if (childNode.outcome === 'forced_draw') {
+385|        outcomeForPlayer = 'forced_draw';
+386|        distForPlayer = null;
+387|      } else {
+388|        outcomeForPlayer = 'not_established';
+389|        distForPlayer = null;
+390|      }
+391|
+392|      return {
+393|        col,
+394|        outcome: outcomeForPlayer,
+395|        distance: distForPlayer,
+396|        childNode
+397|      };
+398|    });
+399|
+400|    let overallOutcome = 'not_established';
+401|    let overallDistance = null;
+402|
+403|    const winChildren = childResultsForPlayer.filter(c => c.outcome === 
'forced_win');
+404|    const lossChildren = childResultsForPlayer.filter(c => c.outcome === 
'forced_loss');
+405|    const drawChildren = childResultsForPlayer.filter(c => c.outcome === 
'forced_draw');
+406|    const unknownChildren = childResultsForPlayer.filter(c => c.outcome ===
'not_established');
+407|
+408|    if (winChildren.length > 0) {
+409|      // Shortest winning distance
+410|      overallOutcome = 'forced_win';
+411|      overallDistance = Math.min(...winChildren.map(c => c.distance));
+412|    } else if (lossChildren.length === childResultsForPlayer.length && 
childResultsForPlayer.length > 0) {
+413|      // All children are proven losses -> longest distance (best 
resistance)
+414|      overallOutcome = 'forced_loss';
+415|      overallDistance = Math.max(...lossChildren.map(c => c.distance));
+416|    } else if (winChildren.length === 0 && unknownChildren.length === 0 && 
drawChildren.length > 0) {
+417|      // No winning, no unknown, at least one draw
+418|      overallOutcome = 'forced_draw';
+419|      overallDistance = null;
+420|    } else {
+421|      overallOutcome = 'not_established';
+422|      overallDistance = null;
+423|    }
+424|
+425|    return {
+426|      id: pathKey,
+427|      columnPath: path,
+428|      lastMove,
+429|      board,
+430|      player,
+431|      perspective: player,
+432|      status: 'active',
+433|      winningCells: [],
+434|      isTerminal: false,
+435|      isHorizon: false,
+436|      outcome: overallOutcome,
+437|      distance: overallDistance,
+438|      children: children.map(c => c.childNode),
+439|      _columnEvaluations: childResultsForPlayer
+440|    };
+441|  }
+442|
+443|  const rootProof = buildProofNode(rootBoard, rootPlayerToMove, maxDepth, 
[], null);
+444|
+445|  const columnSummaries = (rootProof._columnEvaluations || []).map(evalItem
=> {
+446|    let description;
+447|    if (evalItem.outcome === 'forced_win') {
+448|      description = `Forced win in ${evalItem.distance} ${evalItem.distance
=== 1 ? 'ply' : 'plies'}`;
+449|    } else if (evalItem.outcome === 'forced_loss') {
+450|      description = `Forced loss in ${evalItem.distance} 
${evalItem.distance === 1 ? 'ply' : 'plies'}`;
+451|    } else if (evalItem.outcome === 'forced_draw') {
+452|      description = 'Forced draw';
+453|    } else {
+454|      description = `Not established within search depth (${maxDepth} 
${maxDepth === 1 ? 'ply' : 'plies'})`;
+455|    }
+456|
+457|    return {
+458|      column: evalItem.col,
+459|      outcome: evalItem.outcome,
+460|      distance: evalItem.distance,
+461|      description
+462|    };
+463|  });
+464|
+465|  // Clean up private helper properties before returning
+466|  function cleanNode(n) {
+467|    delete n._columnEvaluations;
+468|    if (n.children) {
+469|      n.children.forEach(cleanNode);
+470|    }
+471|  }
+472|  cleanNode(rootProof);
+473|
+474|  return {
+475|    isTerminal: false,
+476|    rootOutcome: null,
+477|    playerToMove: rootPlayerToMove,
+478|    depth: maxDepth,
+479|    columns: columnSummaries,
+480|    tree: rootProof
+481|  };
+482|}
+483|
+484|// Branch Preview and Transplant Logic
+485|function previewTransplant(sourceNode, sourceAllNodes, destNode, 
destAllNodes) {
+486|  // Build lookup maps
+487|  const sourceNodeMap = new Map();
+488|  for (const n of sourceAllNodes) {
+489|    sourceNodeMap.set(n.id, n);
+490|  }
+491|
+492|  const sourceChildrenMap = new Map();
+493|  for (const n of sourceAllNodes) {
+494|    if (n.parent_id) {
+495|      if (!sourceChildrenMap.has(n.parent_id)) {
+496|        sourceChildrenMap.set(n.parent_id, []);
+497|      }
+498|      sourceChildrenMap.get(n.parent_id).push(n);
+499|    }
+500|  }
+501|
+502|  const destChildrenMap = new Map();
+503|  for (const n of destAllNodes) {
+504|    if (n.parent_id) {
+505|      if (!destChildrenMap.has(n.parent_id)) {
+506|        destChildrenMap.set(n.parent_id, []);
+507|      }
+508|      destChildrenMap.get(n.parent_id).push(n);
+509|    }
+510|  }
+511|
+512|  if (!sourceNode.parent_id || sourceNode.incoming_column === null) {
+513|    return {
+514|      valid: false,
+515|      error: {
+516|        path: [],
+517|        reason: 'Cannot transplant from the root position. Choose a branch 
node.'
+518|      }
+519|    };
+520|  }
+521|
+522|  const previewNodes = [];
+523|  let newCount = 0;
+524|  let reusedCount = 0;
+525|
+526|  // Recursive DFS traversal
+527|  function simulateNode(currentSrcNode, parentDestBoard, parentDestTurn, 
parentDestId, relativePath) {
+528|    const col = currentSrcNode.incoming_column;
+529|    const currentPath = [...relativePath, col];
+530|
+531|    // Check if parentDestBoard is terminal
+532|    const parentEval = evaluateBoardState(parentDestBoard);
+533|    if (parentEval.status !== 'active') {
+534|      return {
+535|        valid: false,
+536|        error: {
+537|          path: currentPath,
+538|          reason: `Position is terminal (${parentEval.status})`
+539|        }
+540|      };
+541|    }
+542|
+543|    // Check if column is full
+544|    if (isColumnFull(parentDestBoard, col)) {
+545|      return {
+546|        valid: false,
+547|        error: {
+548|          path: currentPath,
+549|          reason: `Column ${col} is full`
+550|        }
+551|      };
+552|    }
+553|
+554|    // Apply move
+555|    const moveRes = applyMoveToBoard(parentDestBoard, col, parentDestTurn);
+556|    const nextTurn = parentDestTurn === 'Red' ? 'Yellow' : 'Red';
+557|
+558|    // Check if parentDestId has existing child with incoming_column === 
col
+559|    let existingChild = null;
+560|    if (parentDestId && destChildrenMap.has(parentDestId)) {
+561|      existingChild = destChildrenMap.get(parentDestId).find(c => 
c.incoming_column === col);
+562|    }
+563|
+564|    let isReused = false;
+565|    let reusedDestNodeId = null;
+566|
+567|    if (existingChild) {
+568|      isReused = true;
+569|      reusedDestNodeId = existingChild.id;
+570|      reusedCount++;
+571|    } else {
+572|      newCount++;
+573|    }
+574|
+575|    previewNodes.push({
+576|      sourceNodeId: currentSrcNode.id,
+577|      relativePath: currentPath,
+578|      incomingColumn: col,
+579|      color: parentDestTurn,
+580|      landingRow: moveRes.lastMove.row,
+581|      landingCol: moveRes.lastMove.column,
+582|      landingIndex: moveRes.lastMove.index,
+583|      board: moveRes.board,
+584|      turn: nextTurn,
+585|      status: moveRes.status,
+586|      winningCells: moveRes.winningCells,
+587|      reused: isReused,
+588|      reusedDestNodeId: reusedDestNodeId,
+589|      parentSourceId: currentSrcNode.parent_id
+590|    });
+591|
+592|    // Inspect children of currentSrcNode sorted by ascending 
incoming_column
+593|    const srcChildren = (sourceChildrenMap.get(currentSrcNode.id) || [])
+594|      .slice()
+595|      .sort((a, b) => a.incoming_column - b.incoming_column);
+596|
+597|    for (const child of srcChildren) {
+598|      const childRes = simulateNode(
+599|        child,
+600|        moveRes.board,
+601|        nextTurn,
+602|        reusedDestNodeId,
+603|        currentPath
+604|      );
+605|      if (!childRes.valid) {
+606|        return childRes;
+607|      }
+608|    }
+609|
+610|    return { valid: true };
+611|  }
+612|
+613|  const parseBoard = (b) => (typeof b === 'string' ? JSON.parse(b) : b);
+614|  const destBoard = parseBoard(destNode.board);
+615|  const destTurn = destNode.turn;
+616|
+617|  const simResult = simulateNode(sourceNode, destBoard, destTurn, 
destNode.id, []);
+618|  if (!simResult.valid) {
+619|    return {
+620|      valid: false,
+621|      error: simResult.error
+622|    };
+623|  }
+624|
+625|  return {
+626|    valid: true,
+627|    previewNodes,
+628|    newCount,
+629|    reusedCount
+630|  };
+631|}

Summary: Create auth routes and authMiddleware

Reasoning:
**Backend API Route Implementation - Authentication, Competitive Game, and 
Archive**

Alright, let's dive into Task 3: crafting the backend API for authentication, 
the competitive game logic, and the archive routes. I need to make sure I adhere
meticulously to the specifications laid out in `authentication.md`, 
`gameplay.md`, `history.md`, `records.md`, `concurrency.md`, and 
`persistence.md`.

First, the authentication flow. I'll need to define `/api/auth/login`, which 
handles user logins.  This route needs to validate the `email` and `password` 
sent in the request body against the `accounts` table. If there's a mismatch, 
it's a 401 Unauthorized with a clear error message. If the credentials are 
valid, I'll generate a secure, unpredictable bearer token (probably using 
`crypto.randomBytes(32).toString('hex')`) and store it in the `tokens` table. 
The successful response will be the generated `token` along with the user's 
`id`, `email`, and `name`.  Then, `/api/auth/logout` will be responsible for 
invalidating the user's tokens. I'll need to check the `Authorization` header 
for the bearer token, delete all tokens associated with the user, and return a 
success message. The `/api/auth/session` route will allow clients to check for a
valid session, also requiring the bearer token and returning the user's 
information if the token is valid. If invalid or revoked, a 401 is returned.

Next, I'll set up the auth middleware.  I'll extract the bearer token from the 
`Authorization` header, look it up in the `tokens` table joined with the 
`accounts` table. If the token is found, I'll attach the user's data to 
`req.account` and the token itself to `req.token`.  If not, a 401 is returned.

Then, there's the idempotency middleware. This is crucial for handling mutating 
actions and preventing unexpected results. The key is the `operationId`, a 
unique identifier for each action.  I'll also use `expectedRevision` to ensure 
the client has the most up-to-date game state.  For all mutating routes, I'll 
store operation receipts in the `operation_receipts` table. This middleware will
first check the `operation_receipts` table for a record with the same 
`account_id` and `operation_id`. If a record exists, I'll compute a hash of the 
current request input, compare it to the stored `input_hash`. If the hash 
matches, I can immediately return the cached response. If not, a 409 Conflict 
with appropriate error message is returned. When an operation is accepted, I'll 
save the details including the status code and JSON response.

Now, the competitive game logic.  The `/api/game` route will return the current 
state of the game: the `board`, `currentPlayer`, `status` (active, win, draw), 
`winningCells`, scores (`redWins`, `yellowWins`, `draws`), the `appliedHistory`,
`redoHistory`, `revision`, `roundId`, and `canUndo`/`canRedo` flags. The `POST 
/api/game/move` route will handle player moves. It will validate the 
`operationId`, `expectedRevision`, and `column` number. Crucially, I'll ensure 
that the `expectedRevision` matches the current game state to prevent 
conflicting updates, returning a 409 if there's a mismatch. I'll also validate 
the game status and column availability. Once the move is validated, I'll update
the board, history, scores (if applicable), set the `currentPlayer`, increment 
the `revision`, and save the updated game state in a single transaction to the 
`game_states` table, along with the operation receipt. I need to be careful to 
save the completed match in the archive. I'll need a `/api/game/undo` route to 
allow the player to undo a move, which will pop from the `appliedHistory` and 
push it to `redoHistory`. I'll revert any win/draw, decrement the corresponding 
score, delete from the archive, and update the game state, and persist the new 
state, along with a new operation receipt.  The `/api/game/redo` route will 
allow redoing of moves, doing the reverse operations of the `/api/game/undo` 
action. Finally, `/api/game/new` will start a new game by resetting the board 
and initializing the state.

Finally, for the archive routes.  `/api/archive` will provide a list of 
completed matches, showing the latest ten. It returns the total count, along 
with the match ID, result, move

  command: "create"

  file_text: 
    const express = require('express');
    const crypto = require('crypto');
    const { getDb } = require('../db');
    
    const router = express.Router();
    
    // Middleware to authenticate Bearer token
    function authMiddleware(req, res, next) {
      const authHeader = req.headers['authorization'];
      if (!authHeader || !authHeader.startsWith('Bearer ')) {
        return res.status(401).json({ error: 'Authentication required' });
      }
    
      const token = authHeader.slice(7).trim();
      if (!token) {
        return res.status(401).json({ error: 'Authentication required' });
      }
    
      const db = getDb();
      const session = db.prepare(`
        SELECT t.token, a.id, a.email, a.name
        FROM tokens t
        JOIN accounts a ON t.account_id = a.id
        WHERE t.token = ?
      `).get(token);
    
      if (!session) {
        return res.status(401).json({ error: 'Invalid or revoked token' });
      }
    
      req.account = {
        id: session.id,
        email: session.email,
        name: session.name
      };
      req.token = token;
    
      next();
    }
    
    // POST /api/auth/login
    router.post('/login', (req, res) => {
      const { email, password } = req.body || {};
    
      if (!email || !password || typeof email !== 'string' || typeof password 
!== 'string') {
        return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Email and password are required' 
});
      }
    
      const db = getDb();
      const account = db.prepare('SELECT id, email, password, name FROM accounts
WHERE email = ? COLLATE NOCASE').get(email.trim());
    
      if (!account || account.password !== password) {
        return res.status(401).json({ error: 'Invalid email or password' });
      }
    
      const token = crypto.randomBytes(32).toString('hex');
      db.prepare('INSERT INTO tokens (token, account_id) VALUES (?, 
?)').run(token, account.id);
    
      return res.json({
        token,
        account: {
          id: account.id,
          email: account.email,
          name: account.name
        }
      });
    });
    
    // POST /api/auth/logout
    router.post('/logout', authMiddleware, (req, res) => {
      const db = getDb();
      // Revoke this token and all other tokens for this account
      db.prepare('DELETE FROM tokens WHERE account_id = ?').run(req.account.id);
      return res.json({ success: true, message: 'Signed out everywhere' });
    });
    
    // GET /api/auth/session
    router.get('/session', authMiddleware, (req, res) => {
      return res.json({
        account: req.account
      });
    });
    
    module.exports = {
      router,
      authMiddleware
    };

  file_text: 
    const express = require('express');
    const crypto = require('crypto');
    const { getDb } = require('../db');
    
    const router = express.Router();
    
    function authMiddleware(req, res, next) {
      const authHeader = req.headers['authorization'];
      if (!authHeader || !authHeader.startsWith('Bearer ')) {
        return res.status(401).json({ error: 'Authentication required' });
      }
    
      const token = authHeader.slice(7).trim();
      if (!token) {
        return res.status(401).json({ error: 'Authentication required' });
      }
    
      const db = getDb();
      const session = db.prepare(`
        SELECT t.token, a.id, a.email, a.name
        FROM tokens t
        JOIN accounts a ON t.account_id = a.id
        WHERE t.token = ?
      `).get(token);
    
      if (!session) {
        return res.status(401).json({ error: 'Invalid or revoked token' });
      }
    
      req.account = {
        id: session.id,
        email: session.email,
        name: session.name
      };
      req.token = token;
    
      next();
    }
    
    router.post('/login', (req, res) => {
      const { email, password } = req.body || {};
    
      if (!email || !password || typeof email !== 'string' || typeof password 
!== 'string') {
        return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Email and password are required' 
});
      }
    
      const db = getDb();
      const account = db.prepare('SELECT id, email, password, name FROM accounts
WHERE email = ? COLLATE NOCASE').get(email.trim());
    
      if (!account || account.password !== password) {
        return res.status(401).json({ error: 'Invalid email or password' });
      }
    
      const token = crypto.randomBytes(32).toString('hex');
      db.prepare('INSERT INTO tokens (token, account_id) VALUES (?, 
?)').run(token, account.id);
    
      return res.json({
        token,
        account: {
          id: account.id,
          email: account.email,
          name: account.name
        }
      });
    });
    
    router.post('/logout', authMiddleware, (req, res) => {
      const db = getDb();
      db.prepare('DELETE FROM tokens WHERE account_id = ?').run(req.account.id);
      return res.json({ success: true, message: 'Signed out everywhere' });
    });
    
    router.get('/session', authMiddleware, (req, res) => {
      return res.json({
        account: req.account
      });
    });
    
    module.exports = {
      router,
      authMiddleware
    };

Summary: Create operation receipts helper

Reasoning:
**Receipts and Input Hashing Module: Planning and Implementation**

Okay, so I'm thinking we need a dedicated helper module for handling operation 
receipts and input hashing; let's call it `/app/receipts.js`. I need to be 
really precise with this, so let me break it down. Receipts are crucial for 
ensuring idempotency and preventing unintentional operation duplication.

My core idea is that an operation receipt will store data specific to an 
`account_id` and `operation_id`. The critical piece is the `input_hash`, 
essentially a hash or fingerprint of the request parameters, or a normalized 
JSON hash, depending on which way I decide is best.

So, when a request lands, my flow is this: First, check if a receipt already 
exists for that `(account_id, operation_id)` pair. If a receipt *is* found, I 
need to compare the stored `input_hash` against the hash of the current 
request's input. If they match, great! I can simply return the stored `{ 
statusCode, responseJson }`, giving us the expected idempotency.

However, if the `input_hash` values *don't* match, I'm dealing with a conflict –
the operation identifier is being reused with different inputs, which is a big 
no-no. I'll need to return a 409 Conflict error with a clear message: `{ error: 
'Operation identifier reused with different input' }`.

I'll also need helper functions. The `saveReceipt()` function is what I'll use 
to store the receipt data. I imagine the function signature being something like
`saveReceipt(accountId, operationId, scope, inputHash, statusCode, 
responseObj)`. This will insert data into the `operation_receipts` table. 
Finally, I'll need the `hashInput(obj)` function, which will handle the actual 
hashing of the input object.

Alright, time to get to work on writing `/app/receipts.js`.




Action: FileEditorAction

Arguments:
  command: "create"
  path: "/app/receipts.js"
  file_text: 
    const crypto = require('crypto');
    const { getDb } = require('./db');
    
    function hashInput(data) {
      const str = JSON.stringify(data, Object.keys(data || {}).sort());
      return crypto.createHash('sha256').update(str).digest('hex');
    }
    
    function checkReceipt(accountId, operationId, inputHash) {
      if (!operationId) return null;
      const db = getDb();
      const receipt = db.prepare(`
        SELECT status_code, response_json, input_hash
        FROM operation_receipts
        WHERE account_id = ? AND operation_id = ?
      `).get(accountId, operationId);
    
      if (!receipt) return null;
    
      if (receipt.input_hash !== inputHash) {
        return {
          conflict: true,
          statusCode: 409,
          responseJson: { error: 'Operation identifier reused with different 
input' }
        };
      }
    
      return {
        conflict: false,
        statusCode: receipt.status_code,
        responseJson: JSON.parse(receipt.response_json)
      };
    }
    
    function saveReceipt(accountId, operationId, scope, inputHash, statusCode, 
responseObj) {
      if (!operationId) return;
      const db = getDb();
      try {
        db.prepare(`
          INSERT OR REPLACE INTO operation_receipts (
            account_id, operation_id, scope, input_hash, status_code, 
response_json
          ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
        `).run(
          accountId,
          operationId,
          scope,
          inputHash,
          statusCode,
          JSON.stringify(responseObj)
        );
      } catch (err) {
        console.error('Failed to save receipt:', err);
      }
    }
    
    module.exports = {
      hashInput,
      checkReceipt,
      saveReceipt
    };

Summary: Create competitive game and archive routes

Reasoning:
**Game State API Implementation**

Okay, time to get this `/app/routes/game.js` file built for our competitive game
state and match archiving. First, I need to make sure I have all the 
requirements for the competitive game endpoint nailed down.

Let's start with a `GET` request to `/api/game`. This needs to return the 
current game state, which is a hefty object containing the board (an array of 42
strings), the `currentPlayer`, the `status`, and any winning cells. I also need 
to return the win/loss/draw counts (`redWins`, `yellowWins`, `draws`), the 
`appliedHistory`, `redoHistory`, and the game's `revision`, `roundId` and the 
`canUndo` and `canRedo` flags.

Next, the critical `POST /api/game/move` endpoint: This will receive a `column` 
number (1-7) and an `expectedRevision` integer and an operationId. I need to 
handle all the error conditions: a conflict if the `expectedRevision` doesn't 
match the current game's revision (returning a 409 and the authoritative state),
a 400 if the game is already finished, and another 400 if the chosen column is 
full. If everything checks out, I need to apply the move, update the 
`appliedHistory`, and clear the redo stack. If a win or draw is detected, update
the status, increment the win/draw counters, and write this game to the 
`completed_matches` table.  I should save a receipt for all actions, increment 
the revision and then move on.

The `POST /api/game/undo` request is next. It will also need an 
`expectedRevision` integer and an `operationId`. The conflict conditions are the
same as `move`.  If `appliedHistory` is empty, return a 400. Otherwise, undo the
last move, move it to `redoHistory`. If the undo reverses a win or draw, I need 
to revert the score increment, remove the match from the archive (via `DELETE 
FROM completed_matches`), and change the game `status` back to 'active'. Then, 
restore the `board` and `currentPlayer`. I should also save a receipt and 
increment the revision.

Now for `POST /api/game/redo`. I'll again be looking for a matching 
`expectedRevision` and `operationId` and will return a conflict if they don't 
match or a 400 if the `redoHistory` is empty. If successful, I'll restore the 
move from `redoHistory` back to `appliedHistory`. If the re-done move results in
a win/draw, I need to update the score and the `completed_matches` archive (via 
an `INSERT OR REPLACE` to that table). Don't forget to save a receipt and 
increment the revision.

The final endpoint is `POST /api/game/new`, which resets the game, similar to 
when the accounts are created. It takes `expectedRevision` and `operationId` and
will return a conflict if the `expectedRevision` is off. It clears the `board`, 
`appliedHistory`, and `redoHistory`, sets the `status` to 'active', 
`currentPlayer` to 'Red', and generates a new `roundId`. Importantly, I need to 
preserve the `redWins`, `yellowWins`, and `draws` counters. I should increment 
the revision and save the receipt.

Finally, let's not forget the match archive endpoints. `GET /api/archive` 
returns `{ totalCount, matches: [...] }`, where `matches` is the latest 10 
matches (newest first).  And `GET /api/archive/:matchId` returns details about a
specific match: `{ matchId, result, finalBoard, moves, completedAt }`.

Now, before I start coding, I should take a moment and double-check the database
schema for the `completed_matches` table. Specifically the columns: `id, 
account_id, match_id, result, final_board, moves, completed_at`. I'll need to 
remember that `match_id` can be the `round_id`. Also, when undoing a terminal 
move, I'll `DELETE FROM completed_matches WHERE account_id = ? AND match_id = 
?`. When redoing a terminal move, I'll use `INSERT OR REPLACE INTO 
completed_matches ...` with the round_id.

I'll also quickly double-check how I'm formatting the game state data.

Alright, with all that in mind, let's start writing `/app/routes/game.js`.
