+145|// POST /api/analyses - Create new analysis
+146|router.post('/analyses', (req, res) => {
+147|  const accountId = req.account.id;
+148|  const { matchId, step, name, operationId } = req.body || {};
+149|
+150|  const allowedKeys = ['matchId', 'step', 'name', 'operationId'];
+151|  const extraKeys = Object.keys(req.body || {}).filter(k => 
!allowedKeys.includes(k));
+152|  if (extraKeys.length > 0) {
+153|    return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Unexpected additional fields' });
+154|  }
+155|
+156|  if (!operationId || typeof operationId !== 'string' || 
operationId.trim().length === 0) {
+157|    return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Valid operationId is required' 
});
+158|  }
+159|
+160|  if (!matchId || typeof matchId !== 'string') {
+161|    return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Valid matchId is required' });
+162|  }
+163|
+164|  if (typeof step !== 'number' || !Number.isInteger(step) || step < 0) {
+165|    return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Valid integer step is required' 
});
+166|  }
+167|
+168|  if (typeof name !== 'string' || name.trim().length < 1 || 
name.trim().length > 60) {
+169|    return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Name must be between 1 and 60 
characters' });
+170|  }
+171|
+172|  const trimmedName = name.trim();
+173|  const inputHash = hashInput({ action: 'createAnalysis', matchId, step, 
name: trimmedName, operationId });
+174|  const cached = checkReceipt(accountId, operationId, inputHash);
+175|  if (cached) {
+176|    return res.status(cached.statusCode).json(cached.responseJson);
+177|  }
+178|
+179|  const db = getDb();
+180|  const match = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM completed_matches WHERE 
account_id = ? AND match_id = ?').get(accountId, matchId);
+181|
+182|  if (!match) {
+183|    const resp = { error: 'Source completed match not found' };
+184|    saveReceipt(accountId, operationId, 'analysis', inputHash, 404, resp);
+185|    return res.status(404).json(resp);
+186|  }
+187|
+188|  const matchMoves = typeof match.moves === 'string' ? 
JSON.parse(match.moves) : match.moves;
+189|  if (step > matchMoves.length) {
+190|    const resp = { error: `Step ${step} exceeds match length of 
${matchMoves.length}` };
+191|    saveReceipt(accountId, operationId, 'analysis', inputHash, 400, resp);
+192|    return res.status(400).json(resp);
+193|  }
+194|
+195|  const prefix = matchMoves.slice(0, step);
+196|  const derived = engine.deriveStateFromMoves(prefix);
+197|
+198|  const createTx = db.transaction(() => {
+199|    const analysisId = 'analysis-' + crypto.randomUUID();
+200|    const rootNodeId = 'node-' + crypto.randomUUID();
+201|    const now = new Date().toISOString();
+202|
+203|    const lastPrefixMove = prefix.length > 0 ? prefix[prefix.length - 1] : 
null;
+204|
+205|    db.prepare(`
+206|      INSERT INTO analysis_nodes (
+207|        id, analysis_id, account_id, parent_id, incoming_column, 
move_number,
+208|        color, landing_row, landing_col, landing_index, board, turn, 
status, winning_cells, created_at
+209|      ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, NULL, NULL, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
+210|    `).run(
+211|      rootNodeId,
+212|      analysisId,
+213|      accountId,
+214|      prefix.length,
+215|      lastPrefixMove ? lastPrefixMove.color : 'none',
+216|      lastPrefixMove ? lastPrefixMove.row : null,
+217|      lastPrefixMove ? lastPrefixMove.column : null,
+218|      lastPrefixMove ? lastPrefixMove.index : null,
+219|      JSON.stringify(derived.board),
+220|      derived.currentPlayer,
+221|      derived.status,
+222|      JSON.stringify(derived.winningCells),
+223|      now
+224|    );
+225|
+226|    db.prepare(`
+227|      INSERT INTO analyses (
+228|        id, account_id, name, source_match_id, source_step, source_prefix,
+229|        root_node_id, selected_node_id, revision, created_at, updated_at
+230|      ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 0, ?, ?)
+231|    `).run(
+232|      analysisId,
+233|      accountId,
+234|      trimmedName,
+235|      matchId,
+236|      step,
+237|      JSON.stringify(prefix),
+238|      rootNodeId,
+239|      rootNodeId,
+240|      now,
+241|      now
+242|    );
+243|
+244|    const formatted = formatAnalysisFull(db, analysisId, accountId);
+245|    saveReceipt(accountId, operationId, 'analysis', inputHash, 201, 
formatted);
+246|    return formatted;
+247|  });
+248|
+249|  const analysisObj = createTx();
+250|  return res.status(201).json(analysisObj);
+251|});

+253|// GET /api/analyses/:id
+254|router.get('/analyses/:id', (req, res) => {
+255|  const db = getDb();
+256|  const accountId = req.account.id;
+257|  const { id } = req.params;
+258|
+259|  const analysis = formatAnalysisFull(db, id, accountId);
+260|  if (!analysis) {
+261|    return res.status(404).json({ error: 'Analysis not found' });
+262|  }

+267|// POST /api/analyses/:id/move
+268|router.post('/analyses/:id/move', (req, res) => {
+269|  const accountId = req.account.id;
+270|  const { id: analysisId } = req.params;
+271|  const { column, expectedRevision, operationId } = req.body || {};
+272|
+273|  const allowedKeys = ['column', 'expectedRevision', 'operationId'];
+274|  const extraKeys = Object.keys(req.body || {}).filter(k => 
!allowedKeys.includes(k));
+275|  if (extraKeys.length > 0) {
+276|    return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Unexpected additional fields' });
+277|  }
+278|
+279|  if (!operationId || typeof operationId !== 'string' || 
operationId.trim().length === 0) {
+280|    return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Valid operationId is required' 
});
+281|  }
+282|
+283|  if (typeof expectedRevision !== 'number' || 
!Number.isInteger(expectedRevision) || expectedRevision < 0) {
+284|    return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Valid integer expectedRevision is
required' });
+285|  }
+286|
+287|  if (typeof column !== 'number' || !Number.isInteger(column) || column < 1
|| column > engine.COLS) {
+288|    return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Column must be an integer between
1 and 7' });
+289|  }
+290|
+291|  const inputHash = hashInput({ action: 'analysisMove', analysisId, column,
expectedRevision, operationId });
+292|  const cached = checkReceipt(accountId, operationId, inputHash);
+293|  if (cached) {
+294|    return res.status(cached.statusCode).json(cached.responseJson);
+295|  }
+296|
+297|  const db = getDb();
+298|  const performMove = db.transaction(() => {
+299|    const analysis = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM analyses WHERE id = ? AND 
account_id = ?').get(analysisId, accountId);
+300|    if (!analysis) {
+301|      const resp = { error: 'Analysis not found' };
+302|      saveReceipt(accountId, operationId, 'analysis', inputHash, 404, 
resp);
+303|      return { status: 404, body: resp };
+304|    }
+305|
+306|    if (analysis.revision !== expectedRevision) {
+307|      const resp = {
+308|        error: 'Analysis updated in another tab',
+309|        analysis: formatAnalysisFull(db, analysisId, accountId)
+310|      };
+311|      saveReceipt(accountId, operationId, 'analysis', inputHash, 409, 
resp);
+312|      return { status: 409, body: resp };
+313|    }
+314|
+315|    const selectedNode = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM analysis_nodes WHERE id 
= ? AND analysis_id = ?').get(analysis.selected_node_id, analysisId);
+316|    if (!selectedNode) {
+317|      const resp = { error: 'Selected node not found' };
+318|      saveReceipt(accountId, operationId, 'analysis', inputHash, 400, 
resp);
+319|      return { status: 400, body: resp };
+320|    }
+321|
+322|    if (selectedNode.status !== 'active') {
+323|      const resp = { error: 'Cannot make moves in a finished position' };
+324|      saveReceipt(accountId, operationId, 'analysis', inputHash, 400, 
resp);
+325|      return { status: 400, body: resp };
+326|    }
+327|
+328|    const currentBoard = typeof selectedNode.board === 'string' ? 
JSON.parse(selectedNode.board) : selectedNode.board;
+329|    if (engine.isColumnFull(currentBoard, column)) {
+330|      const resp = { error: `Column ${column} is full` };
+331|      saveReceipt(accountId, operationId, 'analysis', inputHash, 400, 
resp);
+332|      return { status: 400, body: resp };
+333|    }
+334|
+335|    // Check if child with this incoming_column already exists from 
selectedNode
+336|    const existingChild = db.prepare(`
+337|      SELECT id FROM analysis_nodes
+338|      WHERE parent_id = ? AND analysis_id = ? AND incoming_column = ?
+339|    `).get(selectedNode.id, analysisId, column);
+340|
+341|    let nextSelectedNodeId;
+342|    const now = new Date().toISOString();
+343|
+344|    if (existingChild) {
+345|      // Reopen existing child
+346|      nextSelectedNodeId = existingChild.id;
+347|    } else {
+348|      // Create new child node
+349|      const moveRes = engine.applyMoveToBoard(currentBoard, column, 
selectedNode.turn);
+350|      const nextTurn = selectedNode.turn === 'Red' ? 'Yellow' : 'Red';
+351|      const newNodeId = 'node-' + crypto.randomUUID();
+352|
+353|      db.prepare(`
+354|        INSERT INTO analysis_nodes (
+355|          id, analysis_id, account_id, parent_id, incoming_column, 
move_number,
+356|          color, landing_row, landing_col, landing_index, board, turn, 
status, winning_cells, created_at
+357|        ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
+358|      `).run(
+359|        newNodeId,
+360|        analysisId,
+361|        accountId,
+362|        selectedNode.id,
+363|        column,
+364|        selectedNode.move_number + 1,
+365|        selectedNode.turn,
+366|        moveRes.lastMove.row,
+367|        column,
+368|        moveRes.lastMove.index,
+369|        JSON.stringify(moveRes.board),
+370|        nextTurn,
+371|        moveRes.status,
+372|        JSON.stringify(moveRes.winningCells),
+373|        now
+374|      );
+375|
+376|      nextSelectedNodeId = newNodeId;
+377|    }
+378|
+379|    const nextRevision = analysis.revision + 1;
+380|    db.prepare(`
+381|      UPDATE analyses SET
+382|        selected_node_id = ?,
+383|        revision = ?,
+384|        updated_at = ?
+385|      WHERE id = ?
+386|    `).run(nextSelectedNodeId, nextRevision, now, analysisId);
+387|
+388|    const updated = formatAnalysisFull(db, analysisId, accountId);
+389|    saveReceipt(accountId, operationId, 'analysis', inputHash, 200, 
updated);
+390|    return { status: 200, body: updated };
+391|  });
+392|
+393|  const resObj = performMove();
+394|  return res.status(resObj.status).json(resObj.body);
+395|});

+468|// POST /api/analyses/:id/redo
+469|router.post('/analyses/:id/redo', (req, res) => {
+470|  const accountId = req.account.id;
+471|  const { id: analysisId } = req.params;
+472|  const { childNodeId, expectedRevision, operationId } = req.body || {};
+473|
+474|  const allowedKeys = ['childNodeId', 'expectedRevision', 'operationId'];
+475|  const extraKeys = Object.keys(req.body || {}).filter(k => 
!allowedKeys.includes(k));
+476|  if (extraKeys.length > 0) {
+477|    return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Unexpected additional fields' });
+478|  }
+479|
+480|  if (!operationId || typeof operationId !== 'string' || 
operationId.trim().length === 0) {
+481|    return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Valid operationId is required' 
});
+482|  }
+483|
+484|  if (typeof expectedRevision !== 'number' || 
!Number.isInteger(expectedRevision) || expectedRevision < 0) {
+485|    return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Valid integer expectedRevision is
required' });
+486|  }
+487|
+488|  const inputHash = hashInput({ action: 'analysisRedo', analysisId, 
childNodeId: childNodeId || null, expectedRevision, operationId });
+489|  const cached = checkReceipt(accountId, operationId, inputHash);
+490|  if (cached) {
+491|    return res.status(cached.statusCode).json(cached.responseJson);
+492|  }
+493|
+494|  const db = getDb();
+495|  const performRedo = db.transaction(() => {
+496|    const analysis = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM analyses WHERE id = ? AND 
account_id = ?').get(analysisId, accountId);
+497|    if (!analysis) {
+498|      const resp = { error: 'Analysis not found' };
+499|      saveReceipt(accountId, operationId, 'analysis', inputHash, 404, 
resp);
+500|      return { status: 404, body: resp };
+501|    }
+502|
+503|    if (analysis.revision !== expectedRevision) {
+504|      const resp = {
+505|        error: 'Analysis updated in another tab',
+506|        analysis: formatAnalysisFull(db, analysisId, accountId)
+507|      };
+508|      saveReceipt(accountId, operationId, 'analysis', inputHash, 409, 
resp);
+509|      return { status: 409, body: resp };
+510|    }
+511|
+512|    const children = db.prepare(`
+513|      SELECT id, incoming_column, color, landing_row, landing_col, 
landing_index
+514|      FROM analysis_nodes
+515|      WHERE parent_id = ? AND analysis_id = ?
+516|      ORDER BY incoming_column ASC
+517|    `).all(analysis.selected_node_id, analysisId);
+518|
+519|    if (children.length === 0) {
+520|      const resp = { error: 'No continuation available to redo' };
+521|      saveReceipt(accountId, operationId, 'analysis', inputHash, 400, 
resp);
+522|      return { status: 400, body: resp };
+523|    }
+524|
+525|    let targetChildId;
+526|    if (children.length === 1 && !childNodeId) {
+527|      targetChildId = children[0].id;
+528|    } else if (childNodeId) {
+529|      const matching = children.find(c => c.id === childNodeId);
+530|      if (!matching) {
+531|        const resp = { error: 'Specified child node is not a direct 
continuation of current node' };
+532|        saveReceipt(accountId, operationId, 'analysis', inputHash, 400, 
resp);
+533|        return { status: 400, body: resp };
+534|      }
+535|      targetChildId = matching.id;
+536|    } else {
+537|      // Multiple children but no child specified
+538|      const resp = {
+539|        error: 'Ambiguous redo continuation. Please choose a child 
branch.',
+540|        availableChildren: children
+541|      };
+542|      saveReceipt(accountId, operationId, 'analysis', inputHash, 400, 
resp);
+543|      return { status: 400, body: resp };
+544|    }
+545|
+546|    const nextRevision = analysis.revision + 1;
+547|    const now = new Date().toISOString();
+548|
+549|    db.prepare(`
+550|      UPDATE analyses SET
+551|        selected_node_id = ?,
+552|        revision = ?,
+553|        updated_at = ?
+554|      WHERE id = ?
+555|    `).run(targetChildId, nextRevision, now, analysisId);
+556|
+557|    const updated = formatAnalysisFull(db, analysisId, accountId);
+558|    saveReceipt(accountId, operationId, 'analysis', inputHash, 200, 
updated);
+559|    return { status: 200, body: updated };
+560|  });
+561|
+562|  const resObj = performRedo();
+563|  return res.status(resObj.status).json(resObj.body);
+564|});
+565|

+870|// POST /api/analyses/:id/transplant/commit

+899|    if (!preview) {

+902|      return { status: 404, body: resp };

+904|

+913|
+914|    if (!sourceAnalysis || !destAnalysis) {
+915|      const resp = { error: 'Source or destination analysis no longer

+916|      saveReceipt(accountId, operationId, 'transplant', inputHash, 404,

+917|      return { status: 404, body: resp };

+919|
+920|    if (sourceAnalysis.revision !== preview.source_revision ||

+923|        sourceRevision: sourceAnalysis.revision,
+924|        destRevision: destAnalysis.revision
+925|      };
+926|      saveReceipt(accountId, operationId, 'transplant', inputHash, 409,

+927|      return { status: 409, body: resp };
+928|    }
+929|
+930|    const previewData = JSON.parse(preview.preview_data);
+931|    const previewNodes = previewData.previewNodes;
+932|
+933|    const destParentNode = db.prepare('SELECT move_number FROM 
analysis_nodes WHERE id = ?').get(preview.dest_node_id);
+934|    const nodeMapping = {};
+935|    const now = new Date().toISOString();
+936|
+937|    for (const node of previewNodes) {
+938|      let actualDestNodeId;
+939|
+940|      if (node.reused) {
+941|        actualDestNodeId = node.reusedDestNodeId;
+942|      } else {
+943|        // Determine parent id in destination
+944|        let parentDestId;
+945|        if (!node.parentSourceId || node.parentSourceId ===

+946|          parentDestId = preview.dest_node_id;
+947|        } else {
+948|          parentDestId = nodeMapping[node.parentSourceId];
+949|        }

+952|        const parentMoveNumber = parentNodeInDest ?

+953|
+954|        const newNodeId = 'node-' + crypto.randomUUID();

+960|        `).run(

+962|          preview.dest_analysis_id,
+963|          accountId,
+964|          parentDestId,
+965|          node.incomingColumn,
+966|          parentMoveNumber + 1,
+967|          node.color,
+968|          node.landingRow,

+1013|// GET or POST /api/analyses/:id/tactical
+1014|function handleTacticalReport(req, res) {
+1015|  const accountId = req.account.id;
+1016|  const { id: analysisId } = req.params;
+1017|  const nodeId = req.method === 'POST' ? req.body?.nodeId :

req.query.depth;
+1019|
+1020|  if (req.method === 'POST') {
+1021|    const allowedKeys = ['nodeId', 'depth'];
+1022|    const extraKeys = Object.keys(req.body || {}).filter(k => 
!allowedKeys.includes(k));
+1023|    if (extraKeys.length > 0) {
+1024|      return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Unexpected additional fields' 
});
+1025|    }
+1026|  }
+1027|
+1028|  if (!nodeId || typeof nodeId !== 'string') {
+1029|    return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Valid nodeId is required' });
+1030|  }
+1031|
+1032|  const depth = Number(rawDepth);
+1033|  if (!Number.isInteger(depth) || depth < 1 || depth > 4) {
+1034|    return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Depth must be an integer between
1 and 4' });
+1035|  }
+1036|
+1037|  const db = getDb();
+1038|  const analysis = db.prepare('SELECT id, revision FROM analyses WHERE id

+1039|  if (!analysis) {
+1040|    return res.status(404).json({ error: 'Analysis not found' });
+1041|  }
+1042|
+1043|  const node = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM analysis_nodes WHERE id = ? AND

+1044|  if (!node) {
+1045|    return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Node not found in this analysis'
});
+1046|  }
+1047|
+1048|  const board = typeof node.board === 'string' ? JSON.parse(node.board) : 
node.board;
+1049|  const winningCells = node.winning_cells === 'none' ||

JSON.parse(node.winning_cells) : node.winning_cells);
+1050|
+1051|  const report = engine.computeTacticalReport(board, node.turn, depth, 
node.status, winningCells);
+1052|

+1054|    analysisId,

+1056|    analysisRevision: analysis.revision,
+1057|    depth,

+1063|  });

Agent Action ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

Summary: Create server.js entrypoint

Reasoning:
**Building the Server: `/app/server.js`**

Okay, time to put it all together. I need to create `/app/server.js`, the 
central piece that brings everything to life. The goal is to get this thing 
running on port 3000, listening on `0.0.0.0`, and serving up the various 
endpoints and static files.

Let's break down the requirements. First, it needs to handle the basics: serving
static files from `/app/public`. Then, I need to define that `/api/health` 
endpoint – a simple GET request that returns `{ status: 'ok' }` with a 200 OK 
status code when the server is ready to go.

Next, the routers. I have to make sure to mount my `authRouter` at `/api/auth`, 
the `gameRouter` at `/api`, and the `analysisRouter` also at `/api`. This is 
critical for organizing the API endpoints.

Finally, and this is important, I need to build in graceful shutdown and handle 
any potential errors with care. No sloppy exits! Now, let's get that 
`/app/server.js` file going.




Action: FileEditorAction

Arguments:
  command: "create"
  path: "/app/server.js"
  file_text: 
    const express = require('express');
    const path = require('path');
    const { getDb, DB_PATH } = require('./db');
    const { router: authRouter } = require('./routes/auth');
    const { router: gameRouter } = require('./routes/game');
    const { router: analysisRouter } = require('./routes/analysis');
    
    const app = express();
    const PORT = process.env.PORT || 3000;
    const HOST = '0.0.0.0';
    
    // Ensure DB is initialized
    getDb();
    
    app.use(express.json());
    
    // Request logging in development/testing if needed
    app.use((req, res, next) => {
      res.setHeader('Cache-Control', 'no-store');
      next();
    });
    
    // Health check endpoint
    app.get('/api/health', (req, res) => {
      res.status(200).json({ status: 'ok', timestamp: new Date().toISOString() 
});
    });
    
    // API Routes
    app.use('/api/auth', authRouter);
    app.use('/api', gameRouter);
    app.use('/api', analysisRouter);
    
    // Serve static assets from public folder
    app.use(express.static(path.join(__dirname, 'public')));
    
    // Fallback to index.html for SPA
    app.get('*', (req, res) => {
      if (req.path.startsWith('/api/')) {
        return res.status(404).json({ error: 'Endpoint not found' });
      }
      res.sendFile(path.join(__dirname, 'public', 'index.html'));
    });
    
    // Start server
    const server = app.listen(PORT, HOST, () => {
      console.log(`DropLine server running at http://${HOST}:${PORT}`);
      console.log(`Using SQLite database at: ${DB_PATH}`);
    });
    
    module.exports = { app, server };

const { app } = require('./server');
const { getDb } = require('./db');

const testPort = 3123;
const server = app.listen(testPort, async () => {
  const baseUrl = 'http://localhost:' + testPort;
  console.log('Testing against ' + baseUrl);

  async function req(path, opts = {}) {
    const res = await fetch(baseUrl + path, {
      headers: {
        'Content-Type': 'application/json',
        ...(opts.token ? { 'Authorization': 'Bearer ' + opts.token } : {}),
        ...opts.headers
      },
      ...opts
    });
    const data = await res.json().catch(() => null);
    return { status: res.status, data };
  }

  try {
    // 1. Health check
    const health = await req('/api/health');
    console.log('Health:', health.status, health.data);
    if (health.status !== 200) throw new Error('Health check failed');

    // 2. Sign in as Jordan (who has completed matches)
    const loginJordan = await req('/api/auth/login', {
      method: 'POST',
      body: JSON.stringify({ email: 'jordan@dropline.test', password: 
'password123' })
    });
    console.log('Login Jordan:', loginJordan.status, loginJordan.data.account);
    const jordanToken = loginJordan.data.token;

    // 3. Archive list
    const archive = await req('/api/archive', { token: jordanToken });
    console.log('Archive matches count:', archive.data.totalCount, 'matches:', 
archive.data.matches.length);
    if (archive.data.totalCount !== 11 || archive.data.matches.length !== 10) {
      throw new Error('Archive list mismatch');
    }

    // 4. Archive detail
    const matchId = archive.data.matches[0].matchId;
    const matchDetail = await req('/api/archive/' + matchId, { token: 
jordanToken });
    console.log('Match detail:', matchDetail.status, matchDetail.data.matchId, 
'moves count:', matchDetail.data.moves.length);

    // 5. Create Analysis from step 3 of this match
    const createAnalysis = await req('/api/analyses', {
      method: 'POST',
      token: jordanToken,
      body: JSON.stringify({
        matchId: matchId,
        step: 3,
        name: 'Jordan Opening Study',
        operationId: 'op-create-1'
      })
    });
    console.log('Create analysis status:', createAnalysis.status, 'Analysis 
ID:', createAnalysis.data?.id, 'name:', createAnalysis.data?.name);
    if (createAnalysis.status !== 201) throw new Error('Create analysis 
failed');
    const analysisId = createAnalysis.data.id;
    let rev = createAnalysis.data.revision;

    // 6. Test Idempotent exact retry of analysis creation
    const retryCreate = await req('/api/analyses', {
      method: 'POST',
      token: jordanToken,
      body: JSON.stringify({
        matchId: matchId,
        step: 3,
        name: 'Jordan Opening Study',
        operationId: 'op-create-1'
      })
    });
    console.log('Retry create status:', retryCreate.status, 'same id:', 
retryCreate.data.id === analysisId);
    if (retryCreate.status !== 201 || retryCreate.data.id !== analysisId) {
      throw new Error('Analysis create idempotency failed');
    }

    // 7. Make a move in analysis (col 4)
    const move1 = await req(`/api/analyses/${analysisId}/move`, {
      method: 'POST',
      token: jordanToken,
      body: JSON.stringify({
        column: 4,
        expectedRevision: rev,
        operationId: 'op-move-1'
      })
    });
    console.log('Analysis move 1 status:', move1.status, 'new revision:', 
move1.data.revision);
    if (move1.status !== 200) throw new Error('Analysis move 1 failed');
    rev = move1.data.revision;
    const nodeAfterMove1 = move1.data.selectedNode.id;

    // 8. Undo analysis move
    const undo1 = await req(`/api/analyses/${analysisId}/undo`, {
      method: 'POST',
      token: jordanToken,
      body: JSON.stringify({
        expectedRevision: rev,
        operationId: 'op-undo-1'
      })
    });
    console.log('Analysis undo 1 status:', undo1.status, 'new revision:', 
undo1.data.revision);
    if (undo1.status !== 200) throw new Error('Analysis undo failed');
    rev = undo1.data.revision;

    // 9. Make an alternative move (col 5) from root
    const move2 = await req(`/api/analyses/${analysisId}/move`, {
      method: 'POST',
      token: jordanToken,
      body: JSON.stringify({
        column: 5,
        expectedRevision: rev,
        operationId: 'op-move-2'
      })
    });
    console.log('Analysis move 2 status:', move2.status, 'new revision:', 
move2.data.revision);
    if (move2.status !== 200) throw new Error('Analysis move 2 failed');
    rev = move2.data.revision;
    const nodeAfterMove2 = move2.data.selectedNode.id;

    // 10. Undo again to root
    const undo2 = await req(`/api/analyses/${analysisId}/undo`, {
      method: 'POST',
      token: jordanToken,
      body: JSON.stringify({
        expectedRevision: rev,
        operationId: 'op-undo-2'
      })
    });
    rev = undo2.data.revision;

    // 11. Redo with ambiguity check (root now has 2 children: col 4 and col 5)
    const ambiguousRedo = await req(`/api/analyses/${analysisId}/redo`, {
      method: 'POST',
      token: jordanToken,
      body: JSON.stringify({
        expectedRevision: rev,
        operationId: 'op-redo-ambig'
      })
    });
    console.log('Ambiguous redo status (expected 400):', ambiguousRedo.status, 
ambiguousRedo.data.error);
    if (ambiguousRedo.status !== 400) throw new Error('Expected 400 for 
ambiguous redo');

    // 12. Redo specifying child node (nodeAfterMove1)
    const chosenRedo = await req(`/api/analyses/${analysisId}/redo`, {
      method: 'POST',
      token: jordanToken,
      body: JSON.stringify({
        childNodeId: nodeAfterMove1,
        expectedRevision: rev,
        operationId: 'op-redo-chosen'
      })
    });
    console.log('Chosen redo status:', chosenRedo.status, 'selected node:', 
chosenRedo.data.selectedNode.id === nodeAfterMove1);
    if (chosenRedo.status !== 200) throw new Error('Chosen redo failed');
    rev = chosenRedo.data.revision;

    // 13. Compare nodeAfterMove1 and nodeAfterMove2
    const compare = await req(`/api/analyses/${analysisId}/compare`, {
      method: 'POST',
      token: jordanToken,
      body: JSON.stringify({
        nodeAId: nodeAfterMove1,
        nodeBId: nodeAfterMove2
      })
    });
    console.log('Compare status:', compare.status, 'shared moves:', 
compare.data.sharedOpeningMovesCount, 'diff cells:', 
compare.data.differingCells.length);
    if (compare.status !== 200 || compare.data.sharedOpeningMovesCount !== 3 || 
compare.data.differingCells.length !== 2) {
      throw new Error('Comparison result mismatch');
    }

    // 14. Tactical Report on nodeAfterMove1 (depth 2)
    const tactical = await 
req(`/api/analyses/${analysisId}/tactical?nodeId=${nodeAfterMove1}&depth=2`, {
      token: jordanToken
    });
    console.log('Tactical report status:', tactical.status, 'columns length:', 
tactical.data.columns.length, 'tree root outcome:', tactical.data.tree.outcome);
    if (tactical.status !== 200 || tactical.data.columns.length !== 7) {
      throw new Error('Tactical report failed');
    }

    // 15. Preview branch transplant from nodeAfterMove1 to nodeAfterMove2
    const preview = await req(`/api/analyses/${analysisId}/transplant/preview`, 
{
      method: 'POST',
      token: jordanToken,
      body: JSON.stringify({
        sourceNodeId: nodeAfterMove1,
        destAnalysisId: analysisId,
        destNodeId: nodeAfterMove2
      })
    });
    console.log('Transplant preview status:', preview.status, 'newCount:', 
preview.data.newCount, 'reusedCount:', preview.data.reusedCount);
    if (preview.status !== 200) throw new Error('Transplant preview failed');

    // 16. Commit branch transplant
    const commitTransplant = await 
req(`/api/analyses/${analysisId}/transplant/commit`, {
      method: 'POST',
      token: jordanToken,
      body: JSON.stringify({
        previewId: preview.data.previewId,
        operationId: 'op-transplant-commit-1'
      })
    });
    console.log('Transplant commit status:', commitTransplant.status, 
'nodeMapping:', commitTransplant.data.nodeMapping);
    if (commitTransplant.status !== 200) throw new Error('Transplant commit 
failed');

    console.log('ALL BACKEND INTEGRATION TESTS PASSED PERFECTLY!');
  } catch (err) {
    console.error('Test error:', err);
  } finally {
    server.close();
    process.exit(0);
  }
});

Summary: Create test_backend.js

Reasoning:
Okay, here's my interpretation of those thoughts, tailored for an expert 
audience, as if they were my own:

**Refactoring the Test Execution**

Right, that bash string interpolation `-e " ... ${analysisId} ... "` is biting 
me. It's expanding *before* Node even gets a crack at it, which is obviously not
what I intended. It's too tightly coupled with the shell.

The fix, I think, is a dedicated test script. Let's create 
`/app/test_backend.js` for this. Then, we can run it cleanly with `node 
test_backend.js`. This isolates the test logic, cleans up the command, and lets 
Node handle the execution of the test environment variables, which will be much 
easier to reason about and debug than what I had. This approach is standard 
practice, really, and should make the tests more portable and less prone to 
unexpected shell-related behaviors. It will also allow for better separation of 
concerns between shell scripting (if any remains) and the test execution itself.
It's a cleaner separation, and the tests will be simpler to debug.

    const { app } = require('./server');
    const { getDb } = require('./db');
    
    const testPort = 3123;
    const server = app.listen(testPort, async () => {
      const baseUrl = 'http://localhost:' + testPort;
      console.log('Testing against ' + baseUrl);
    
      async function req(path, opts = {}) {
        const res = await fetch(baseUrl + path, {
          headers: {
            'Content-Type': 'application/json',
            ...(opts.token ? { 'Authorization': 'Bearer ' + opts.token } : {}),
            ...opts.headers
          },
          ...opts
        });
        const data = await res.json().catch(() => null);
        return { status: res.status, data };
      }
    
      try {
        // 1. Health check
        const health = await req('/api/health');
        console.log('Health:', health.status, health.data);
        if (health.status !== 200) throw new Error('Health check failed');
    
        // 2. Sign in as Jordan (who has completed matches)
        const loginJordan = await req('/api/auth/login', {
          method: 'POST',
          body: JSON.stringify({ email: 'jordan@dropline.test', password: 
'password123' })
        });
        console.log('Login Jordan:', loginJordan.status, 
loginJordan.data.account);
        const jordanToken = loginJordan.data.token;
    
        // 3. Archive list
        const archive = await req('/api/archive', { token: jordanToken });
        console.log('Archive matches count:', archive.data.totalCount, 
'matches:', archive.data.matches.length);
        if (archive.data.totalCount !== 11 || archive.data.matches.length !== 
10) {
          throw new Error('Archive list mismatch');
        }
    
        // 4. Archive detail
        const matchId = archive.data.matches[0].matchId;
        const matchDetail = await req('/api/archive/' + matchId, { token: 
jordanToken });
        console.log('Match detail:', matchDetail.status, 
matchDetail.data.matchId, 'moves count:', matchDetail.data.moves.length);
    
        // 5. Create Analysis from step 3 of this match
        const createAnalysis = await req('/api/analyses', {
          method: 'POST',
          token: jordanToken,
          body: JSON.stringify({
            matchId: matchId,
            step: 3,
            name: 'Jordan Opening Study',
            operationId: 'op-create-1'
          })
        });
        console.log('Create analysis status:', createAnalysis.status, 'Analysis 
ID:', createAnalysis.data?.id, 'name:', createAnalysis.data?.name);
        if (createAnalysis.status !== 201) throw new Error('Create analysis 
failed: ' + JSON.stringify(createAnalysis.data));
        const analysisId = createAnalysis.data.id;
        let rev = createAnalysis.data.revision;
    
        // 6. Test Idempotent exact retry of analysis creation
        const retryCreate = await req('/api/analyses', {
          method: 'POST',
          token: jordanToken,
          body: JSON.stringify({
            matchId: matchId,
            step: 3,
            name: 'Jordan Opening Study',
            operationId: 'op-create-1'
          })
        });
        console.log('Retry create status:', retryCreate.status, 'same id:', 
retryCreate.data.id === analysisId);
        if (retryCreate.status !== 201 || retryCreate.data.id !== analysisId) {
          throw new Error('Analysis create idempotency failed');
        }
    
        // 7. Make a move in analysis (col 4)
        const move1 = await req(`/api/analyses/${analysisId}/move`, {
          method: 'POST',
          token: jordanToken,
          body: JSON.stringify({
            column: 4,
            expectedRevision: rev,
            operationId: 'op-move-1'
          })
        });
        console.log('Analysis move 1 status:', move1.status, 'new revision:', 
move1.data.revision);
        if (move1.status !== 200) throw new Error('Analysis move 1 failed: ' + 
JSON.stringify(move1.data));
        rev = move1.data.revision;
        const nodeAfterMove1 = move1.data.selectedNode.id;
    
        // 8. Undo analysis move
        const undo1 = await req(`/api/analyses/${analysisId}/undo`, {
          method: 'POST',
          token: jordanToken,
          body: JSON.stringify({
            expectedRevision: rev,
            operationId: 'op-undo-1'
          })
        });
        console.log('Analysis undo 1 status:', undo1.status, 'new revision:', 
undo1.data.revision);
        if (undo1.status !== 200) throw new Error('Analysis undo failed');
        rev = undo1.data.revision;
    
        // 9. Make an alternative move (col 5) from root
        const move2 = await req(`/api/analyses/${analysisId}/move`, {
          method: 'POST',
          token: jordanToken,
          body: JSON.stringify({
            column: 5,
            expectedRevision: rev,
            operationId: 'op-move-2'
          })
        });
        console.log('Analysis move 2 status:', move2.status, 'new revision:', 
move2.data.revision);
        if (move2.status !== 200) throw new Error('Analysis move 2 failed');
        rev = move2.data.revision;
        const nodeAfterMove2 = move2.data.selectedNode.id;
    
        // 10. Undo again to root
        const undo2 = await req(`/api/analyses/${analysisId}/undo`, {
          method: 'POST',
          token: jordanToken,
          body: JSON.stringify({
            expectedRevision: rev,
            operationId: 'op-undo-2'
          })
        });
        rev = undo2.data.revision;
    
        // 11. Redo with ambiguity check (root now has 2 children: col 4 and col
5)
        const ambiguousRedo = await req(`/api/analyses/${analysisId}/redo`, {
          method: 'POST',
          token: jordanToken,
          body: JSON.stringify({
            expectedRevision: rev,
            operationId: 'op-redo-ambig'
          })
        });
        console.log('Ambiguous redo status (expected 400):', 
ambiguousRedo.status, ambiguousRedo.data.error);
        if (ambiguousRedo.status !== 400) throw new Error('Expected 400 for 
ambiguous redo');
    
        // 12. Redo specifying child node (nodeAfterMove1)
        const chosenRedo = await req(`/api/analyses/${analysisId}/redo`, {
          method: 'POST',
          token: jordanToken,
          body: JSON.stringify({
            childNodeId: nodeAfterMove1,
            expectedRevision: rev,
            operationId: 'op-redo-chosen'
          })
        });
        console.log('Chosen redo status:', chosenRedo.status, 'selected node:', 
chosenRedo.data.selectedNode.id === nodeAfterMove1);
        if (chosenRedo.status !== 200) throw new Error('Chosen redo failed');
        rev = chosenRedo.data.revision;
    
        // 13. Compare nodeAfterMove1 and nodeAfterMove2
        const compare = await req(`/api/analyses/${analysisId}/compare`, {
          method: 'POST',
          token: jordanToken,
          body: JSON.stringify({
            nodeAId: nodeAfterMove1,
            nodeBId: nodeAfterMove2
          })
        });
        console.log('Compare status:', compare.status, 'shared moves:', 
compare.data.sharedOpeningMovesCount, 'diff cells:', 
compare.data.differingCells.length);
        if (compare.status !== 200 || compare.data.sharedOpeningMovesCount !== 3
|| compare.data.differingCells.length !== 2) {
          throw new Error('Comparison result mismatch');
        }
    
        // 14. Tactical Report on nodeAfterMove1 (depth 2)
        const tactical = await 
req(`/api/analyses/${analysisId}/tactical?nodeId=${nodeAfterMove1}&depth=2`, {
          token: jordanToken
        });
        console.log('Tactical report status:', tactical.status, 'columns 
length:', tactical.data.columns.length, 'tree root outcome:', 
tactical.data.tree.outcome);
        if (tactical.status !== 200 || tactical.data.columns.length !== 7) {
          throw new Error('Tactical report failed');
        }
    
        // 15. Preview branch transplant from nodeAfterMove1 to nodeAfterMove2
        const preview = await 
req(`/api/analyses/${analysisId}/transplant/preview`, {
          method: 'POST',
          token: jordanToken,
          body: JSON.stringify({
            sourceNodeId: nodeAfterMove1,
            destAnalysisId: analysisId,
            destNodeId: nodeAfterMove2
          })
        });
        console.log('Transplant preview status:', preview.status, 'newCount:', 
preview.data.newCount, 'reusedCount:', preview.data.reusedCount);
        if (preview.status !== 200) throw new Error('Transplant preview failed: 
' + JSON.stringify(preview.data));
    
        // 16. Commit branch transplant
        const commitTransplant = await 
req(`/api/analyses/${analysisId}/transplant/commit`, {
          method: 'POST',
          token: jordanToken,
          body: JSON.stringify({
            previewId: preview.data.previewId,
            operationId: 'op-transplant-commit-1'
          })
        });
        console.log('Transplant commit status:', commitTransplant.status, 
'nodeMapping:', commitTransplant.data.nodeMapping);
        if (commitTransplant.status !== 200) throw new Error('Transplant commit 
failed: ' + JSON.stringify(commitTransplant.data));
    
        console.log('=== ALL BACKEND INTEGRATION TESTS PASSED PERFECTLY! ===');
      } catch (err) {
        console.error('Test error:', err);
        process.exit(1);
      } finally {
        server.close();
        process.exit(0);
      }
    });

Agent Action ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

Summary: Fix Express 5 wildcard routing syntax

Reasoning:
Okay, here's what's going through my mind about this Express situation:

**Refactoring the Express Wildcard Route**

Alright, so I'm taking a look at this Express application and it seems like it's
using an older approach to wildcard routes. Specifically, it's leveraging 
`path-to-regexp` v8's `*` wildcard directly within the `app.get()` route 
definition, like `app.get('{*path}', ...)`, or falling back to middleware, via 
`app.use((req, res) => ...)`. This approach works, sure, but it's not the 
cleanest or most idiomatic way to handle this in modern Express.

The more standard and generally preferred method, in my opinion, is to implement
the wildcard route as a fallback middleware handler. This aligns better with 
Express's intended architecture, providing better control over the routing logic
and allowing for clearer separation of concerns.

Therefore, my immediate thought is to refactor this wildcard route. The ideal 
solution here would be to convert it to using the `app.use((req, res) => ...)` 
approach in `/app/server.js`, assuming the application architecture allows for 
it. That should keep the application cleaner and easier to maintain in the long 
run. Let's get that done!




Action: FileEditorAction

Arguments:
  command: "str_replace"
  path: "/app/server.js"
  old_str: 
    // Fallback to index.html for SPA
    app.get('*', (req, res) => {
      if (req.path.startsWith('/api/')) {
        return res.status(404).json({ error: 'Endpoint not found' });
      }
      res.sendFile(path.join(__dirname, 'public', 'index.html'));
    });

  new_str: 
    // Fallback to index.html for SPA
    app.use((req, res) => {
      if (req.path.startsWith('/api/')) {
        return res.status(404).json({ error: 'Endpoint not found' });
      }
      res.sendFile(path.join(__dirname, 'public', 'index.html'));
    });

  kind: "FileEditorAction"

Agent Action ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

Summary: Insert analyses record before analysis_nodes to satisfy foreign key

Reasoning:
**Debugging a Tricky Database Insertion Order Issue**

Okay, let's dive into this `createTx` failure in `routes/analysis.js`. The error
has to be in this `INSERT INTO analysis_nodes` statement. Let's examine the SQL:

```sql
INSERT INTO analysis_nodes (
  id, analysis_id, account_id, parent_id, incoming_column, move_number,
  color, landing_row, landing_col, landing_index, board, turn, status, 
winning_cells, created_at
) VALUES (?, ?, ?, NULL, NULL, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
```

Hmm, first thought, let's make sure the number of column placeholders matches 
the listed columns. I see: `id`, `analysis_id`, `account_id`, `parent_id` (which
is a `NULL` literal, not a placeholder), `incoming_column` (also a `NULL` 
literal), then `move_number`, `color`, and so on... Counting, I've got 15 
columns. And in the `VALUES` clause, I count 3 question marks for `id`, 
`analysis_id`, `account_id` then two NULLs, then 10 more question marks for the 
rest. So, 13 question marks, total! Then I check how many args are passed: 
`rootNodeId`, `analysisId`, `accountId`, and then a bunch more that add up to 
13. Excellent. Looks like the SQL and arguments line up.

Wait, hold on a second! Let's check foreign keys. *analyses* table has a foreign
key to *accounts* table and then *analysis_nodes* has a foreign key referencing 
the *analyses* table, specifically the `id` column. Aha! I remember that! In my 
database schema, `analysis_nodes` references `analyses`. And here's the problem:
in `createTx`, we are inserting into `analysis_nodes` *before* inserting into 
`analyses`. Since I've got `db.pragma('foreign_keys = ON')`, this means a 
foreign key constraint violation is going to happen! Boom! That's the error! We 
are trying to insert a child record before the parent!

Now, the solution is obvious: we *must* insert into `analyses` first, and *then*
insert into `analysis_nodes`. Or, we need to restructure the insertion logic 
within the transaction. I remember `analyses` table also has `root_node_id` and 
`selected_node_id`, which are text IDs, and not foreign keys. So, inserting into
`analyses` first, then `analysis_nodes` will be perfect.

Okay, time to go back to `routes/analysis.js` and fix the insertion order in 
`createTx` and anywhere else this pattern is used. Good catch!

playback mechanism feels intuitive. The "Create Analysis" feature is a great 
idea.

Finally, the analysis workspace. This is where users can *really* dig deep. I 
need a robust interface that allows seamless navigation between "Competitive 
Match" and "Saved Analyses". The analyses list needs to be clear, offering quick
actions. Within an analysis, I must build a flexible, powerful environment that 
supports exploration. The analysis board, variation tree viewer, selected line 
history, and particularly the analysis tools are paramount. The tools will be 
powerful; I will implement node comparisons, branch transplants, and tactical 
reports. The reports are quite complex and powerful; I need to make them usable.

Across the entire application, I'll apply visual styling, adhering to modern UI 
design principles with high contrast. The colors must be carefully chosen to 
distinguish and the focus styling (outlines) must be precise. I must ensure full
keyboard reachability, responsive design that works on all devices, and respect 
the user's preference for reduced motion. The goal is to build a beautiful, 
accessible, and highly functional application. This will take some work, but I 
am excited to dig in.




Action: FileEditorAction

  command: "create"
  path: "/app/public/index.html"

Agent Action ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

Summary: Create style.css design system

Reasoning:
**CSS Design Considerations**

Okay, time to get this stylesheet, `/app/public/style.css`, built. I need to 
make sure this CSS really *sings*. First and foremost, the aesthetic has to 
scream "clean, professional board game." Think elegant, not cluttered.

Accessibility is paramount, of course. Contrast ratios *must* hit WCAG AAA / AA 
standards – no compromises. I'm going with a strong deep crimson (`#dc2626`) and
gold (`#eab308`) for the tokens; those colors are classic board game colors. The
winning cell is going to be handled with more than just color; I'll need a 
thick, glowing gold/white outline, *plus* an icon or label for clarity. No 
relying solely on color to convey that critical information. For the comparison 
boards, I need to make the diff markers distinct, using something like a simple 
`[Δ]` symbol, coupled with a non-color pattern or border, and clear text labels.

Responsiveness is also key. We're going full responsive support down to 375px 
wide. No horizontal scrolling, none of that. I'll make sure boards stack, tables
become responsive, and flex grids are used where necessary. That’s 
non-negotiable.

Don't forget the focus states; strong focus outlines with `:focus-visible` are 
crucial for keyboard navigation. Finally, gotta respect the users' preferences: 
I'll include `prefers-reduced-motion: reduce` to disable transitions and 
animations when requested. Alright, let's get writing.




Action: FileEditorAction

Arguments:
  command: "create"
  path: "/app/public/style.css"
  file_text:

Agent Action ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

Summary: Create client-side application app.js

Reasoning:
**Client-Side JavaScript Logic: App.js - Deep Dive**

Okay, time to build out the client-side JavaScript for the application, 
specifically within `/app/public/app.js`. Let's break down the core 
functionalities and carefully architect them.

First, the **State & Session** management is crucial. I'll need to define how 
the application holds and maintains its state. I will ensure data is persistent 
appropriately. This includes things like:

*   `currentToken`: This will be stashed in either `localStorage` or 
`sessionStorage` for authentication.
*   `currentAccount`: This will hold user details - `id`, `email`, and `name`.
*   `gameState`: This needs to accurately reflect the state of the competitive 
game, of course.
*   `archiveState`: Will need to track the list of previous matches along with 
their metadata.
*   `openReplay`: This will store details about the current match being 
replayed, including its current step.
*   `analysesList`: Keep a list of saved analyses.
*   `openAnalysis`: Will hold details of the currently open analysis, including 
revision, treeNodes, and the selected node.
*   `isPending`: This will be a boolean to prevent duplicate requests when a 
request is in flight.

Second, I will set up an **Idempotency Key Generation** with 
`generateOperationId()` and make sure this is unique, using UUID generation, or 
providing a fallback.

Third, for **Screen Reader Announcement**, I need a mechanism 
(`announce(message)`) to set the text content of an element like 
`#sr-announcements` for accessibility.

Fourth, the **Authentication Flow** is paramount. I'll need to define the logic 
for:

*   **On Load**: Check for a token. If found, test the `/api/auth/session` 
endpoint. If valid, show the app workspace, load the game, and hide the sign-in 
screen. Otherwise, clear the token and revert to the sign-in screen.
*   **Sign-In Form Submit**: Submit to `/api/auth/login` with email and 
password. Handle errors gracefully (visible error alert), and on success, store 
the token, display the account name/email, and load the workspace.
*   **Sign-Out Button**: Make a call to `/api/auth/logout`. Clear the token and 
state, and show the sign-in screen.

Fifth, let's address the **Competitive Game Board Rendering & Interactions**. 
This is where the core game play will reside:

*   **42-Cell Grid Rendering**: This will be the foundational element, ensuring 
the grid has exactly 42 cells. Each cell will require an accessible name format:
"Row R, Column C, OCCUPANT," (or "winning" if the cell is part of a winning 
sequence). Pieces will be rendered as Red, Yellow, or empty. Winning cells will 
require visual indicators like `.winning-cell` and `.winning-badge`.
*   **Column Drop Controls**: Buttons will be used (1 through 7). Keyboard 
navigation is essential (`keydown` events on the buttons, including `ArrowLeft`,
`ArrowRight`, `Home`, `End`, `Enter`/`Space`). Focus retention must be handled. 
Pending guard is key - disable inputs while waiting for the next operation to 
complete.
*   **Move/Undo/Redo/New Game**: Include `operationId` and `expectedRevision: 
gameState.revision` with each move request. Handle 409 Conflict responses (game 
updated in another tab). Update turn indicator, scores, revision badge, history 
list, and undo/redo button availability.

Sixth, for **Move History Rendering**, I will display a list of moves in the 
current round, formatted like "Move N: Color in column C, row R".

Seventh, the **Archive & Read-only Replay** features:

*   **Archive List Table**: Show the total match count and the latest 10 
matches. A "Replay" button will open the replay panel.
*   **Replay**: Fetch the match details by `matchId`. Implement a slider 
(`min="0"`, `max="moves.length"`, `value="currentStep"`) for navigation. 
Previous/Next buttons are needed. Reconstruct the board from `moves.slice(0, 
currentStep)`. A 42-cell replay grid with accessible labels is essential.
*   **"Create Analysis from this Step" Form**: This form needs an input name 
field. It will `POST /api/analyses` with `matchId`, `step`, `name`, and 
`operationId`. On success, open the newly created analysis immediately.

Finally, the **Analysis Workspace**, a much more complex area, and it's 
essential to get it right:

*   **Analyses List**: Render
