+830|  async function openAnalysisById(analysisId) {
+831|    const res = await apiRequest(`/api/analyses/${analysisId}`);
+832|    if (res.ok && res.data) {
+833|      openAnalysis = res.data;
+834|      analysesListView.classList.add('hidden');
+835|      analysisWorkspaceView.classList.remove('hidden');
+836|      renderOpenAnalysis();
+837|      switchStudyTab('tree');
+838|      announce(`Opened analysis "${openAnalysis.name}"`);
+839|    }
+840|  }
+841|
+842|  function renderOpenAnalysis() {
+843|    if (!openAnalysis) return;
+844|
+845|    openAnalysisName.textContent = openAnalysis.name;
+846|    analysisSourceLabel.textContent = `Source: 
${openAnalysis.sourceMatchId} (Step ${openAnalysis.sourceStep})`;
+847|    analysisRevisionBadge.textContent = `Revision: 
${openAnalysis.revision}`;
+848|
+849|    const sel = openAnalysis.selectedNode;
+850|
+851|    // Status Indicator
+852|    analysisStatusIndicator.className = 'turn-indicator';
+853|    if (sel.status === 'active') {
+854|      analysisStatusIndicator.textContent = `${sel.turn}'s turn`;
+855|      analysisStatusIndicator.classList.add(sel.turn === 'Red' ? 'turn-red'
: 'turn-yellow');
+856|    } else if (sel.status === 'Red wins') {
+857|      analysisStatusIndicator.textContent = 'Red wins';
+858|      analysisStatusIndicator.classList.add('turn-red');
+859|    } else if (sel.status === 'Yellow wins') {
+860|      analysisStatusIndicator.textContent = 'Yellow wins';
+861|      analysisStatusIndicator.classList.add('turn-yellow');
+862|    } else if (sel.status === 'Draw') {
+863|      analysisStatusIndicator.textContent = 'Draw';
+864|      analysisStatusIndicator.classList.add('turn-draw');
+865|    }
+866|
+867|    // Board Grid
+868|    renderGrid(analysisGrid, sel.board, sel.winningCells);
+869|
+870|    // Undo / Redo
+871|    analysisUndoBtn.disabled = !sel.canUndo || isPending;
+872|    analysisRedoBtn.disabled = !sel.canRedo || isPending;
+873|
+874|    // Hide choice panel by default
+875|    redoChoicePanel.classList.add('hidden');
+876|
+877|    // Render History & Tree
+878|    renderAnalysisHistory();
+879|    renderVariationTree();
+880|
+881|    // Populate comparison & tactical dropdowns
+882|    populateCompareSelects();
+883|    populateTransplantSelects();
+884|    populateTacticalSelects();
+885|  }
+886|
+887|  function renderAnalysisHistory() {
+888|    analysisHistoryList.innerHTML = '';
+889|    if (!openAnalysis.fullSelectedHistory || 
openAnalysis.fullSelectedHistory.length === 0) {
+890|      analysisHistoryList.innerHTML = '<p class="empty-hint">At root step 
with no prior moves.</p>';
+891|      return;
+892|    }
+893|
+894|    openAnalysis.fullSelectedHistory.forEach((m, idx) => {
+895|      const item = document.createElement('div');
+896|      item.className = `history-item ${m.color === 'Red' ? 
'history-item-red' : 'history-item-yellow'}`;
+897|      item.setAttribute('role', 'listitem');
+898|
+899|      item.innerHTML = `
+900|        <span class="history-move-num">Move ${idx + 1}</span>
+901|        <span class="history-move-detail">${m.color} in column ${m.column},
row ${m.row}</span>
+902|      `;
+903|      analysisHistoryList.appendChild(item);
+904|    });
+905|
+906|    analysisHistoryList.scrollTop = analysisHistoryList.scrollHeight;
+907|  }
+908|
+909|  function renderVariationTree() {
+910|    variationTreeContainer.innerHTML = '';
+911|    if (!openAnalysis.treeNodes || openAnalysis.treeNodes.length === 0) 
return;
+912|
+913|    // Build hierarchical tree from root
+914|    const nodeMap = new Map(openAnalysis.treeNodes.map(n => [n.id, n]));
+915|    const root = nodeMap.get(openAnalysis.rootNodeId);
+916|
+917|    function renderSubtree(node, depth) {
+918|      const row = document.createElement('div');
+919|      row.className = `tree-node-item ${node.id === 
openAnalysis.selectedNodeId ? 'selected' : ''}`;
+920|      row.setAttribute('role', 'treeitem');
+921|      row.setAttribute('tabindex', '0');
+922|
+923|      let labelText = '';
+924|      if (!node.parentId) {
+925|        labelText = `[Root] Step ${openAnalysis.sourceStep} (${node.turn}'s
turn)`;
+926|      } else {
+927|        labelText = `Move ${node.moveNumber}: ${node.color} in col 
${node.incomingColumn}, row ${node.landingRow}`;
+928|        if (node.status !== 'active') {
+929|          labelText += ` [${node.status}]`;
+930|        }
+931|      }
+932|
+933|      row.setAttribute('aria-label', labelText + (node.id === 
openAnalysis.selectedNodeId ? ' (selected)' : ''));
+934|
+935|      // Indent
+936|      const indentSpan = document.createElement('span');
+937|      indentSpan.innerHTML = '&nbsp;'.repeat(depth * 4);
+938|      row.appendChild(indentSpan);
+939|
+940|      // Dot
+941|      const dot = document.createElement('span');
+942|      dot.className = `tree-node-color-dot ${!node.parentId ? 'dot-root' : 
(node.color === 'Red' ? 'dot-red' : 'dot-yellow')}`;
+943|      row.appendChild(dot);
+944|
+945|      // Text
+946|      const textSpan = document.createElement('span');
+947|      textSpan.textContent = labelText;
+948|      row.appendChild(textSpan);
+949|
+950|      row.addEventListener('click', () => 
handleSelectAnalysisNode(node.id));
+951|      row.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
+952|        if (e.key === 'Enter' || e.key === ' ') {
+953|          e.preventDefault();
+954|          handleSelectAnalysisNode(node.id);
+955|        }
+956|      });
+957|
+958|      variationTreeContainer.appendChild(row);
+959|
+960|      const childNodes = (node.childrenIds || [])
+961|        .map(cid => nodeMap.get(cid))
+962|        .filter(Boolean)
+963|        .sort((a, b) => a.incomingColumn - b.incomingColumn);
+964|
+965|      childNodes.forEach(child => renderSubtree(child, depth + 1));
+966|    }
+967|
+968|    if (root) {
+969|      renderSubtree(root, 0);
+970|    }
+971|  }
+972|
+973|  function showAnalysisFeedback(msg, isError = true) {
+974|    if (!msg) {
+975|      analysisFeedbackAlert.classList.add('hidden');
+976|      return;
+977|    }
+978|    analysisFeedbackAlert.textContent = msg;
+979|    analysisFeedbackAlert.className = `alert ${isError ? 'alert-error' : 
'alert-warning'}`;
+980|    analysisFeedbackAlert.classList.remove('hidden');
+981|    announce(msg);
+982|  }
+983|
+984|  async function handleAnalysisColumnDrop(col) {
+985|    if (isPending || !openAnalysis) return;
+986|    showAnalysisFeedback('');
+987|
+988|    isPending = true;
+989|    const opId = generateUUID();
+990|    const res = await apiRequest(`/api/analyses/${openAnalysis.id}/move`, 
'POST', {
+991|      column: col,
+992|      expectedRevision: openAnalysis.revision,
+993|      operationId: opId
+994|    });
+995|
+996|    isPending = false;
+997|
+998|    if (res.ok && res.data) {
+999|      openAnalysis = res.data;
+1000|      renderOpenAnalysis();
+1001|      announce(`Placed piece in column ${col}.`);
+1002|    } else if (res.status === 409) {
+1003|      if (res.data?.analysis) openAnalysis = res.data.analysis;
+1004|      renderOpenAnalysis();
+1005|      showAnalysisFeedback('Analysis updated in another tab');
+1006|    } else {
+1007|      showAnalysisFeedback(res.data?.error || `Column ${col} is full`);
+1008|      renderOpenAnalysis();
+1009|    }
+1010|  }
+1011|
+1012|  async function handleAnalysisUndo() {
+1013|    if (isPending || !openAnalysis || !openAnalysis.selectedNode.canUndo) 
return;

+1019|      expectedRevision: openAnalysis.revision,

+1030|      if (res.data?.analysis) openAnalysis = res.data.analysis;
+1031|      renderOpenAnalysis();
+1032|      showAnalysisFeedback('Analysis updated in another tab');

+1038|
+1039|  async function handleAnalysisRedo(childId = null) {
+1040|    if (isPending || !openAnalysis || !openAnalysis.selectedNode.canRedo) 
return;
+1041|    showAnalysisFeedback('');
+1042|
+1043|    const children = openAnalysis.selectedNode.availableChildren || [];
+1044|
+1045|    // If multiple children and no child chosen, show choice UI
+1046|    if (children.length > 1 && !childId) {
+1047|      redoChoiceButtons.innerHTML = '';
+1048|      children.forEach(c => {
+1049|        const btn = document.createElement('button');
+1050|        btn.className = 'btn btn-primary btn-sm';
+1051|        btn.textContent = `Col ${c.incomingColumn} (${c.color})`;
+1052|        btn.setAttribute('aria-label', `Choose continuation column 
${c.incomingColumn}`);
+1053|        btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
+1054|          redoChoicePanel.classList.add('hidden');
+1055|          handleAnalysisRedo(c.id);
+1056|        });
+1057|        redoChoiceButtons.appendChild(btn);
+1058|      });
+1059|      redoChoicePanel.classList.remove('hidden');
+1060|      redoChoiceButtons.querySelector('button')?.focus();
+1061|      return;
+1062|    }
+1063|
+1064|    isPending = true;
+1065|    const opId = generateUUID();
+1066|    const body = {
+1067|      expectedRevision: openAnalysis.revision,
+1068|      operationId: opId
+1069|    };
+1070|    if (childId) body.childNodeId = childId;
+1071|
+1072|    const res = await apiRequest(`/api/analyses/${openAnalysis.id}/redo`, 
'POST', body);
+1073|    isPending = false;
+1074|
+1075|    if (res.ok && res.data) {
+1076|      openAnalysis = res.data;
+1077|      renderOpenAnalysis();
+1078|      announce('Analysis redo: selected child continuation.');
+1079|    } else if (res.status === 409) {
+1080|      if (res.data?.analysis) openAnalysis = res.data.analysis;
+1081|      renderOpenAnalysis();
+1082|      showAnalysisFeedback('Analysis updated in another tab');
+1083|    } else {
+1084|      showAnalysisFeedback(res.data?.error || 'Redo failed');
+1085|      renderOpenAnalysis();
+1086|    }
+1087|  }

+1154|  function populateCompareSelects() {
+1155|    if (!openAnalysis || !openAnalysis.treeNodes) return;
+1156|    const currentA = compareNodeASelect.value;
+1157|    const currentB = compareNodeBSelect.value;
+1158|
+1159|    compareNodeASelect.innerHTML = '';
+1160|    compareNodeBSelect.innerHTML = '';
+1161|
+1162|    openAnalysis.treeNodes.forEach(n => {
+1163|      let desc = n.parentId ? `Move ${n.moveNumber}: ${n.color} in col 
${n.incomingColumn} (Node ${n.id.slice(-6)})` : `[Root] Step 
${openAnalysis.sourceStep}`;
+1164|      const optA = new Option(desc, n.id);
+1165|      const optB = new Option(desc, n.id);
+1166|      compareNodeASelect.appendChild(optA);
+1167|      compareNodeBSelect.appendChild(optB);
+1168|    });
+1169|
+1170|    if (currentA && Array.from(compareNodeASelect.options).some(o => 
o.value === currentA)) {
+1171|      compareNodeASelect.value = currentA;
+1172|    } else {
+1173|      compareNodeASelect.value = openAnalysis.rootNodeId;
+1174|    }
+1175|
+1176|    if (currentB && Array.from(compareNodeBSelect.options).some(o => 
o.value === currentB)) {
+1177|      compareNodeBSelect.value = currentB;
+1178|    } else {
+1179|      compareNodeBSelect.value = openAnalysis.selectedNodeId;
+1180|    }
+1181|  }

+1183|  async function handleCompareSubmit(e) {
+1184|    e.preventDefault();
+1185|    if (!openAnalysis) return;
+1186|
+1187|    const nodeAId = compareNodeASelect.value;
+1188|    const nodeBId = compareNodeBSelect.value;
+1189|
+1190|    const res = await 
apiRequest(`/api/analyses/${openAnalysis.id}/compare`, 'POST', {
+1191|      nodeAId,
+1192|      nodeBId
+1193|    });
+1194|
+1195|    if (res.ok && res.data) {
+1196|      renderCompareResults(res.data);
+1197|    }
+1198|  }
+1199|
+1200|  function renderCompareResults(data) {
+1201|    compareResultBox.classList.remove('hidden');
+1202|
+1203|    compareSummaryBadge.textContent = `Shared Opening Moves: 
${data.sharedOpeningMovesCount} • Differing Cells: 
${data.differingCells.length}`;
+1204|
+1205|    compareBoardATitle.textContent = `Position A (Move 
${data.nodeA.moveNumber}, ${data.nodeA.turn}'s turn)`;
+1206|    compareBoardBTitle.textContent = `Position B (Move 
${data.nodeB.moveNumber}, ${data.nodeB.turn}'s turn)`;
+1207|
+1208|    renderGrid(compareGridA, data.nodeA.board, data.nodeA.winningCells, 
data.differingCells);
+1209|    renderGrid(compareGridB, data.nodeB.board, data.nodeB.winningCells, 
data.differingCells);
+1210|
+1211|    compareDiffList.innerHTML = '';
+1212|    if (data.differingCells.length === 0) {
+1213|      compareDiffList.innerHTML = '<p class="empty-hint">Both positions 
have identical boards.</p>';
+1214|    } else {
+1215|      data.differingCells.forEach(d => {
+1216|        const item = document.createElement('div');
+1217|        item.className = 'diff-item';
+1218|        item.innerHTML = `
+1219|          <span><strong>Row ${d.row}, Col ${d.column}</strong> (Cell 
${d.index})</span>
+1220|          <span>A: <em>${d.occupantA}</em> vs B: 
<em>${d.occupantB}</em></span>
+1221|        `;
+1222|        compareDiffList.appendChild(item);
+1223|      });
+1224|    }
+1225|
+1226|    announce(`Comparison computed: ${data.sharedOpeningMovesCount} shared 
moves, ${data.differingCells.length} differences.`);
+1227|  }

+1231|    compareNodeASelect.value = compareNodeBSelect.value;
+1232|    compareNodeBSelect.value = temp;
+1233|    compareForm.requestSubmit();
+1234|  }

+1240|  function populateTransplantSelects() {
+1241|    if (!openAnalysis || !openAnalysis.treeNodes) return;
+1242|
+1243|    // Source Branch: non-root nodes
+1244|    transplantSourceNodeSelect.innerHTML = '';
+1245|    const nonRootNodes = openAnalysis.treeNodes.filter(n => n.parentId);
+1246|    if (nonRootNodes.length === 0) {
+1247|      transplantSourceNodeSelect.appendChild(new Option('(No branch moves 
in this analysis)', ''));
+1248|      transplantSourceNodeSelect.disabled = true;
+1249|    } else {
+1250|      transplantSourceNodeSelect.disabled = false;
+1251|      nonRootNodes.forEach(n => {
+1252|        const desc = `Move ${n.moveNumber}: Col ${n.incomingColumn} 
(${n.color}) [Node ${n.id.slice(-6)}]`;
+1253|        transplantSourceNodeSelect.appendChild(new Option(desc, n.id));
+1254|      });
+1255|    }
+1256|
+1257|    // Destination Analysis: all saved analyses
+1258|    transplantDestAnalysisSelect.innerHTML = '';
+1259|    analysesList.forEach(a => {
+1260|      const desc = `${a.name} (Rev ${a.revision})`;
+1261|      transplantDestAnalysisSelect.appendChild(new Option(desc, a.id));
+1262|    });
+1263|    transplantDestAnalysisSelect.value = openAnalysis.id;
+1264|
+1265|    updateDestNodeSelect();
+1266|  }
+1267|
+1268|  async function updateDestNodeSelect() {
+1269|    const destAnalysisId = transplantDestAnalysisSelect.value;
+1270|    if (!destAnalysisId) return;
+1271|
+1272|    let targetAnalysis = openAnalysis;
+1273|    if (destAnalysisId !== openAnalysis.id) {
+1274|      const res = await apiRequest(`/api/analyses/${destAnalysisId}`);
+1275|      if (res.ok && res.data) targetAnalysis = res.data;
+1276|    }
+1277|
+1278|    transplantDestNodeSelect.innerHTML = '';
+1279|    (targetAnalysis.treeNodes || []).forEach(n => {
+1280|      const desc = n.parentId ? `Move ${n.moveNumber}: Col 
${n.incomingColumn} (${n.color})` : `[Root] Step ${targetAnalysis.sourceStep}`;
+1281|      transplantDestNodeSelect.appendChild(new Option(desc, n.id));
+1282|    });
+1283|  }

+1285|  async function handleTransplantPreview(e) {
+1286|    e.preventDefault();
+1287|    if (!openAnalysis) return;
+1288|    transplantFeedback.classList.add('hidden');
+1289|    transplantPreviewResultBox.classList.add('hidden');

+1297|      transplantFeedback.textContent = 'Please choose valid source branch 
and destination.';
+1298|      transplantFeedback.classList.remove('hidden');
+1299|      return;

apiRequest(`/api/analyses/${openAnalysis.id}/transplant/preview`, 'POST', {
+1303|      sourceNodeId,
+1304|      destAnalysisId,
+1305|      destNodeId
+1306|    });
+1307|

+1309|      lastTransplantPreview = res.data;
+1310|      renderTransplantPreview(res.data);
+1311|    } else {
+1312|      let errMsg = res.data?.error || 'Transplant preview failed.';
+1313|      if (res.data?.illegalPath) {
+1314|        errMsg += ` (Illegal at path: [${res.data.illegalPath.join(', 
')}])`;
+1315|      }
+1316|      transplantFeedback.textContent = errMsg;
+1317|      transplantFeedback.classList.remove('hidden');
+1318|      announce(errMsg);
+1319|    }
+1320|  }

+1343|  async function handleTransplantCommit() {
+1344|    if (!lastTransplantPreview || !openAnalysis) return;
+1345|    transplantFeedback.classList.add('hidden');
+1346|
+1347|    const opId = generateUUID();
+1348|    const res = await 
apiRequest(`/api/analyses/${openAnalysis.id}/transplant/commit`, 'POST', {
+1349|      previewId: lastTransplantPreview.previewId,
+1350|      operationId: opId
+1351|    });
+1352|
+1353|    if (res.ok && res.data) {
+1354|      transplantPreviewResultBox.classList.add('hidden');
+1355|      transplantCommitResultBox.textContent = `Transplant committed 
successfully! Created ${res.data.newCount} nodes, reused ${res.data.reusedCount}
nodes.`;
+1356|      transplantCommitResultBox.classList.remove('hidden');
+1357|      announce(transplantCommitResultBox.textContent);
+1358|
+1359|      // Reload analysis
+1360|      await openAnalysisById(openAnalysis.id);
+1361|      await loadAnalysesList();
+1362|    } else if (res.status === 409) {
+1363|      transplantFeedback.textContent = res.data?.error || 'Analysis state 
changed. Please re-preview before committing.';
+1364|      transplantFeedback.classList.remove('hidden');
+1365|      announce(transplantFeedback.textContent);
+1366|    } else {
+1367|      transplantFeedback.textContent = res.data?.error || 'Commit 
failed.';
+1368|      transplantFeedback.classList.remove('hidden');
+1369|      announce(transplantFeedback.textContent);
+1370|    }
+1371|  }

+1394|  async function handleTacticalSubmit(e) {

+1413|  function renderTacticalResults(report) {
+1414|    tacticalResultsBox.classList.remove('hidden');
+1415|
+1416|    tacticalHeaderBadge.textContent = `Search Depth: ${report.depth} 
${report.depth === 1 ? 'ply' : 'plies'} • Perspective: ${report.playerToMove}`;
+1417|
+1418|    tacticalColumnsGrid.innerHTML = '';
+1419|    if (report.isTerminal) {
+1420|      tacticalColumnsGrid.innerHTML = `<div class="card" 
style="grid-column: 1 / -1;"><p>Position is terminal: 
<strong>${report.rootOutcome}</strong>. No playable moves.</p></div>`;
+1421|    } else {
+1422|      report.columns.forEach(colInfo => {
+1423|        const card = document.createElement('div');
+1424|        card.className = 'tactical-col-card';
+1425|
+1426|        let outcomeClass = 'outcome-unknown';
+1427|        if (colInfo.outcome === 'forced_win') outcomeClass = 
'outcome-win';
+1428|        else if (colInfo.outcome === 'forced_loss') outcomeClass = 
'outcome-loss';
+1429|        else if (colInfo.outcome === 'forced_draw') outcomeClass = 
'outcome-draw';
+1430|
+1431|        card.innerHTML = `
+1432|          <div class="tactical-col-header">Column ${colInfo.column}</div>
+1433|          <div class="tactical-outcome 
${outcomeClass}">${colInfo.description}</div>
+1434|        `;
+1435|        tacticalColumnsGrid.appendChild(card);
+1436|      });
+1437|    }
+1438|
+1439|    // Explanation Proof Tree
+1440|    tacticalTreeContainer.innerHTML = '';
+1441|
+1442|    function renderProofSubtree(node, depth) {
+1443|      const row = document.createElement('div');
+1444|      row.className = 'tactical-node-row';
+1445|      row.setAttribute('role', 'treeitem');
+1446|      row.setAttribute('tabindex', '0');
+1447|
+1448|      let outcomeClass = 'outcome-unknown';
+1449|      if (node.outcome === 'forced_win') outcomeClass = 'outcome-win';
+1450|      else if (node.outcome === 'forced_loss') outcomeClass = 
'outcome-loss';
+1451|      else if (node.outcome === 'forced_draw') outcomeClass = 
'outcome-draw';
+1452|
+1453|      let leafTag = '';
+1454|      if (node.isTerminal) leafTag = ' [Terminal Leaf]';
+1455|      else if (node.isHorizon) leafTag = ' [Horizon Leaf]';
+1456|
+1457|      let labelText = '';
+1458|      if (node.columnPath.length === 0) {
+1459|        labelText = `[Root] (${node.player}'s perspective): 
${node.outcome} ${leafTag}`;
+1460|      } else {
+1461|        labelText = `Path [${node.columnPath.join(', ')}] 
(${node.perspective}'s perspective): ${node.outcome}${node.distance ? ' in ' + 
node.distance + ' plies' : ''} ${leafTag}`;
+1462|      }
+1463|
+1464|      row.setAttribute('aria-label', labelText);
+1465|
+1466|      const indentSpan = document.createElement('span');
+1467|      indentSpan.innerHTML = '&nbsp;'.repeat(depth * 4);
+1468|      row.appendChild(indentSpan);
+1469|
+1470|      const dot = document.createElement('span');
+1471|      dot.className = `tree-node-color-dot ${node.player === 'Red' ? 
'dot-red' : 'dot-yellow'}`;
+1472|      row.appendChild(dot);
+1473|
+1474|      const textSpan = document.createElement('span');
+1475|      textSpan.className = outcomeClass;
+1476|      textSpan.textContent = labelText;
+1477|      row.appendChild(textSpan);
+1478|
+1479|      row.addEventListener('click', () => openProofInspector(node));
+1480|      row.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
+1481|        if (e.key === 'Enter' || e.key === ' ') {
+1482|          e.preventDefault();
+1483|          openProofInspector(node);
+1484|        }
+1485|      });
+1486|
+1487|      tacticalTreeContainer.appendChild(row);
+1488|
+1489|      (node.children || []).forEach(child => renderProofSubtree(child, 
depth + 1));
+1490|    }
+1491|
+1492|    if (report.tree) {
+1493|      renderProofSubtree(report.tree, 0);
+1494|    }
+1495|
+1496|    announce(`Tactical report generated for depth ${report.depth}`);
+1497|  }
+1498|
+1499|  function openProofInspector(proofNode) {
+1500|    proofNodeMeta.textContent = `Relative Path:

Arguments:
  command: "create"
  path: "/app/APP_MANIFEST.md"
  file_text: 
    # DropLine Application Manifest
    
    ## Start Command
    ```bash
    node /app/server.js
    ```
    
    ## Database
    SQLite path: /app/dropline.db
    
    Environment variable `DB_PATH` is honored if provided; otherwise defaults to
`/app/dropline.db`.
    
    ## Health Check
    - `GET /api/health` — Returns server health status `{ "status": "ok", 
"timestamp": "..." }`.
    
    ## Authentication Routes
    - `POST /api/auth/login` — Sign in with email and password. Returns bearer 
token and account details.
    - `POST /api/auth/logout` — Revoke active token and all other tokens for the
signed-in account (signs out everywhere).
    - `GET /api/auth/session` — Get current authenticated session account 
information.
    
    ## Competitive Game Routes
    - `GET /api/game` — Read the current account's active Connect Four board, 
turn/result, winning markers, match scores, applied move history, redo 
availability, and server revision.
    - `POST /api/game/move` — Drop a piece in a column (1–7) with 
`expectedRevision` and `operationId`. Validates gravity, alternating turns, 
full-column rejection, win and draw detection, and records completed matches in 
the archive upon game end.
    - `POST /api/game/undo` — Undo the most recent move with `expectedRevision` 
and `operationId`. Reverses score increments and removes completed matches from 
the archive if undoing a terminal move.
    - `POST /api/game/redo` — Redo the previously undone move with 
`expectedRevision` and `operationId`. Re-applies score increments and archive 
records if redoing a terminal move.
    - `POST /api/game/new` — Clear current round board, history, and redo stack 
to start a new game starting with Red's turn, preserving match totals.
    
    ## Completed Match Archive & Replay Routes
    - `GET /api/archive` — Get total completed match count and the latest 10 
matches (newest first) with result, move count, and timestamp.
    - `GET /api/archive/:matchId` — Fetch a completed match's full move sequence
and final board for read-only replay.
    
    ## Branching Analysis Routes
    - `GET /api/analyses` — List all private saved analyses for the 
authenticated account.
    - `POST /api/analyses` — Create a new named branching analysis frozen at a 
specific replay step of a completed match, with `matchId`, `step`, `name`, and 
`operationId`.
    - `GET /api/analyses/:id` — Read full analysis workspace data, including 
root snapshot, selected node board, turn, result, winning markers, full 
selected-line history, available continuations, and the variation tree.
    - `POST /api/analyses/:id/move` — Drop a piece on the analysis practice 
board at the current selected node with `column`, `expectedRevision`, and 
`operationId`. Reopens existing children if the same column is played again from
the same parent.
    - `POST /api/analyses/:id/undo` — Select parent node in the variation tree 
with `expectedRevision` and `operationId`.
    - `POST /api/analyses/:id/redo` — Select direct child continuation node in 
the variation tree with `expectedRevision`, `operationId`, and optional 
`childNodeId` (required when ambiguous).
    - `POST /api/analyses/:id/select` — Select any position node in the 
variation tree with `nodeId`, `expectedRevision`, and `operationId`.
    - `POST /api/analyses/:id/rename` — Rename the analysis with `name` (1–60 
chars trimmed), `expectedRevision`, and `operationId`.
    - `POST /api/analyses/:id/compare` — Compare any two nodes in the analysis 
(`nodeAId`, `nodeBId`). Returns both complete boards, shared opening moves 
count, and exact differing cells with row/col coordinates.
    
    ## Study Tools: Branch Transplant & Tactical Reports
    - `POST /api/analyses/:id/transplant/preview` — Preview transplanting a 
branch (`sourceNodeId`) onto any destination node (`destAnalysisId`, 
`destNodeId`). Re-evaluates gravity, alternating turns, wins, and draws afresh; 
reports relative column paths, new node count, reused node count, or first 
deterministic illegal move.
    - `POST /api/analyses/:id/transplant/commit` — Atomically commit a validated
preview into the destination analysis with `previewId` and `operationId`.
    - `GET /api/analyses/:id/tactical` & `POST /api/analyses/:id/tactical` — Run
bounded minimax tactical search from player-to-move's perspective for depths 1 
through 4 plies. Returns forced win/loss/draw/not established recommendations 
for every legal column, along with a browsable explanation proof tree and 
42-cell position inspector data.
    

  kind: "FileEditorAction"

Summary: Create comprehensive end-to-end test suite

Reasoning:
**My Testing To-Do List**

Okay, time to craft a really robust test suite in `/app/test_suite.js`. I need 
to ensure everything works flawlessly. Here's a rundown of what needs rigorous 
testing:

First, **Account Management**: I'll cover the sign-in process, testing invalid 
credentials, correct token issuance, and making sure that multi-token sign-out 
revokes everywhere.

Second, **Account Isolation**: Avery and Jordan, they need to be completely 
isolated. Their boards, revisions, scores, game history, archives, analyses, and
matches shouldn't overlap. Jordan absolutely cannot mess with Avery's stuff.

Third, the **Competitive Connect Four Rules**: This section needs extreme 
detail! I'll be testing:
*   Alternating turns for Red and Yellow.
*   The gravity mechanics, ensuring pieces drop correctly to the lowest empty 
cell.
*   Full column rejections ("Column N is full").
*   Win detection: horizontal, vertical, and both diagonals.
*   Draw detection on a full board without a winner.
*   Terminal locking: moves are rejected after a win or draw.
*   Score updates for Red wins, Yellow wins, and Draws.
*   Undo/Redo functionality: moves are moved/restored correctly, terminal states
are handled (active play after undoing win/draw; archive updates on undo/redo), 
new moves clear the redo stack, and new games reset everything, while scores 
persist.

Next, **Concurrency, Revisions & Idempotency** will be tested:
*   Testing `expectedRevision` returning a 409 Conflict with `"Game updated in 
another tab"` when it's wrong, and the current state.
*   Sending the same `operationId` with the same input should return a cached 
response without any state change.
*   The same `operationId` with different input must trigger a 409 Conflict.

Fifth, **Analysis Creation & Branching**:
*   I'll test creating named analyses from steps in replay.
*   Analysis name trimming (between 1 and 60 characters), rejecting blank or 
overlong names with a 400 error.
*   The variation tree structure: adding moves creates nodes, undo selects 
parent nodes, and redo selects child nodes.
*   Branching to see that new moves after undo maintain alternative paths.
*   Ambiguous redo: A 400 error if there are multiple children, and providing 
the `childNodeId` when it's necessary.
*   Reopening existing child nodes when playing the same column from the same 
parent.
*   Tree selection: Selecting any node restores the exact board, turn, and game 
status.
*   Renaming analyses.
*   Ensuring practice moves/wins/draws *never* affect the competitive board, 
scores, or archive.

Then, **Analysis Comparison** will be thoroughly examined:
*   Comparing a node to itself will return a common prefix of the full length 
and zero differences.
*   Comparing two different nodes to ensure common prefix lengths and differing 
cells are identified correctly.
*   Making sure that swapping the order of the comparison nodes doesn't break 
anything.
*   Comparison operations should never change analysis revision or save state.

After that, **Branch Transplant Preview & Commit** will be tested with:
*   Choosing the source root must be rejected with 400.
*   Re-evaluating columns on the destination with gravity, turns, and terminal 
checks.
*   Detecting illegal moves (like full columns) and reporting the first 
deterministic illegal path.
*   Valid previews returning new and reused counts.
*   Committing the preview, advancing the destination revision, creating nodes, 
preserving cursors, and returning the `nodeMapping`.
*   Stale commits (where source or destination revision has changed) resulting 
in a 409 Conflict.
*   Ensuring that a committed preview cannot be re-committed.

Finally, **Bounded Tactical Reports** will cover:
*   Testing depths 1, 2, 3, and 4 plies.
*   Invalid depths (0, 5, "two", 2.5) returning 400 errors.
*   Immediate win detection at 1 ply.
*   Defense/loss distance reporting.
*   Testing a browsable explanation tree with the relative column path, 
perspective, outcome, and distance.
*   A terminal root returning a result with no playable moves.

It's going to be an intense but comprehensive suite! This should provide me with
the peace of mind that everything functions correctly.




Action: FileEditorAction

Arguments:
  command: "create"
  path: "/app/test_suite.js"
  file_text: 
    const { app } = require('./server');
    const { getDb } = require('./db');
    
    const testPort = 3125;
    const server = app.listen(testPort, async () => {
      const baseUrl = `http://localhost:${testPort}`;
      console.log(`Starting DropLine Full Test Suite against ${baseUrl}...`);
    
      let testsPassed = 0;
      let testsFailed = 0;
    
      function assert(condition, message) {
        if (!condition) {
          console.error(`❌ FAILED: ${message}`);
          testsFailed++;
          throw new Error(message);
        } else {
          console.log(`✓ PASSED: ${message}`);
          testsPassed++;
        }
      }
    
      async function req(path, opts = {}) {
        const res = await fetch(baseUrl + path, {
          method: opts.method || 'GET',
          headers: {
            'Content-Type': 'application/json',
            ...(opts.token ? { 'Authorization': 'Bearer ' + opts.token } : {}),
            ...opts.headers
          },
          body: opts.body ? JSON.stringify(opts.body) : undefined
        });
        const data = await res.json().catch(() => null);
        return { status: res.status, ok: res.ok, data };
      }
    
      try {
        // -------------------------------------------------------------
        // Test 1: Health Check
        // -------------------------------------------------------------
        const health = await req('/api/health');
        assert(health.status === 200 && health.data.status === 'ok', 'Health 
check returns 200 ok');
    
        // -------------------------------------------------------------
        // Test 2: Authentication & Multi-Token Sign-out
        // -------------------------------------------------------------
        const badLogin = await req('/api/auth/login', {
          method: 'POST',
          body: { email: 'avery@dropline.test', password: 'wrongpassword' }
        });
        assert(badLogin.status === 401, 'Bad credentials return 401');
    
        const loginAvery1 = await req('/api/auth/login', {
          method: 'POST',
          body: { email: 'avery@dropline.test', password: 'password123' }
        });
        assert(loginAvery1.status === 200 && loginAvery1.data.token, 'Avery 
login 1 succeeds');
        const tokenAvery1 = loginAvery1.data.token;
    
        const loginAvery2 = await req('/api/auth/login', {
          method: 'POST',
          body: { email: 'avery@dropline.test', password: 'password123' }
        });
        assert(loginAvery2.status === 200 && loginAvery2.data.token, 'Avery 
login 2 succeeds (second session)');
        const tokenAvery2 = loginAvery2.data.token;
    
        const loginJordan = await req('/api/auth/login', {
          method: 'POST',
          body: { email: 'jordan@dropline.test', password: 'password123' }
        });
        assert(loginJordan.status === 200 && loginJordan.data.token, 'Jordan 
login succeeds');
        const tokenJordan = loginJordan.data.token;
    
        // Check sessions
        const sessAvery1 = await req('/api/auth/session', { token: tokenAvery1 
});
        assert(sessAvery1.status === 200 && sessAvery1.data.account.name === 
'Avery Morgan', 'Session 1 is valid');
    
        // Sign out Avery session 1 -> should revoke session 2 as well
        const logoutRes = await req('/api/auth/logout', { method: 'POST', token:
tokenAvery1 });
        assert(logoutRes.status === 200, 'Logout succeeds');
    
        const sessAvery2 = await req('/api/auth/session', { token: tokenAvery2 
});
        assert(sessAvery2.status === 401, 'Signing out revoked token everywhere 
for Avery');
    
        const sessJordan = await req('/api/auth/session', { token: tokenJordan 
});
        assert(sessJordan.status === 200, 'Jordan session remains unaffected by 
Avery logout');
    
        // Re-login Avery for remaining tests
        const reloginAvery = await req('/api/auth/login', {
          method: 'POST',
          body: { email: 'avery@dropline.test', password: 'password123' }
        });
        const averyToken = reloginAvery.data.token;
    
        // -------------------------------------------------------------
        // Test 3: Account Isolation & Seed Initial States
        // -------------------------------------------------------------
        const averyGame = await req('/api/game', { token: averyToken });
        const jordanGame = await req('/api/game', { token: tokenJordan });
    
        assert(averyGame.data.redWins === 2 && averyGame.data.yellowWins === 1 
&& averyGame.data.draws === 1, 'Avery seed scores loaded correctly (2, 1, 1)');
        assert(jordanGame.data.redWins === 1 && jordanGame.data.yellowWins === 2
&& jordanGame.data.draws === 0, 'Jordan seed scores loaded correctly (1, 2, 
0)');
        assert(averyGame.data.appliedHistory.length === 2 && 
averyGame.data.redoHistory.length === 1, 'Avery has 2 applied moves and 1 redo 
move');
        assert(jordanGame.data.appliedHistory.length === 3 && 
jordanGame.data.redoHistory.length === 0, 'Jordan has 3 applied moves and 0 redo
moves');
    
        const averyArchive = await req('/api/archive', { token: averyToken });
        const jordanArchive = await req('/api/archive', { token: tokenJordan });
        assert(averyArchive.data.totalCount === 0, 'Avery has 0 archived 
matches');
        assert(jordanArchive.data.totalCount === 11, 'Jordan has 11 archived 
matches');
    
        // Jordan cannot read Avery's data or vice versa
        const foreignMatch = await req('/api/archive/' + 
jordanArchive.data.matches[0].matchId, { token: averyToken });
        assert(foreignMatch.status === 404, 'Avery cannot access Jordan archive 
match');
    
        // -------------------------------------------------------------
        // Test 4: Competitive Game Mutations, Concurrency & Idempotency
        // -------------------------------------------------------------
        let averyRev = averyGame.data.revision;
    
        // Stale revision conflict test
        const staleMove = await req('/api/game/move', {
          method: 'POST',
          token: averyToken,
          body: { column: 4, expectedRevision: averyRev - 1, operationId: 
'stale-op-1' }
        });
        assert(staleMove.status === 409 && staleMove.data.error === 'Game 
updated in another tab', 'Stale expectedRevision returns 409 with authoritative 
state');
    
        // Valid move
        const moveOpId = 'valid-move-op-1';
        const moveRes = await req('/api/game/move', {
          method: 'POST',
          token: averyToken,
          body: { column: 4, expectedRevision: averyRev, operationId: moveOpId }
        });
        assert(moveRes.status === 200 && moveRes.data.revision === averyRev + 1,
'Valid move advances revision by 1');
        averyRev = moveRes.data.revision;
    
        // Idempotent retry of same move
        const retryMoveRes = await req('/api/game/move', {
          method: 'POST',
          token: averyToken,
          body: { column: 4, expectedRevision: averyRev - 1, operationId: 
moveOpId }
        });
        assert(retryMoveRes.status === 200 && retryMoveRes.data.revision === 
averyRev, 'Idempotent retry with same opId returns original response without 
second mutation');
    
        // Reusing opId with different input returns conflict
        const reuseOpRes = await req('/api/game/move', {
          method: 'POST',
          token: averyToken,
          body: { column: 5, expectedRevision: averyRev, operationId: moveOpId }
        });
        assert(reuseOpRes.status === 409, 'Reusing operationId with different 
input returns 409 conflict');
    
        // -------------------------------------------------------------
        // Test 5: Competitive Connect Four Win, Score Increment & Undo
        // -------------------------------------------------------------
        // Start clean game for Avery to test full win flow
        const newGameRes = await req('/api/game/new', {
          method: 'POST',
          token: averyToken,
          body: { expectedRevision: averyRev, operationId: 'new-game-test-1' }
        });
        assert(newGameRes.status === 200 && newGameRes.data.currentPlayer === 
'Red', 'New game resets to Red turn');
        averyRev = newGameRes.data.revision;
        const preWinRedScore = newGameRes.data.redWins;
    
        // Play vertical win for Red in column 1:
        // Red 1, Yellow 2, Red 1, Yellow 2, Red 1, Yellow 2, Red 1 (Win!)
        const moves = [
          { col: 1 }, { col: 2 },
          { col: 1 }, { col: 2 },
          { col: 1 }, { col: 2 },
          { col: 1 }
        ];
    
        let lastMoveRes;
        for (let i = 0; i < moves.length; i++) {
          lastMoveRes = await req('/api/game/move', {
            method: 'POST',
            token: averyToken,
            body: { column: moves[i].col, expectedRevision: averyRev, 
operationId: `win-flow-${i}` }
          });
          averyRev = lastMoveRes.data.revision;
        }
    
        assert(lastMoveRes.data.status === 'Red wins', 'Red vertical win 
detected');
        assert(lastMoveRes.data.winningCells.length === 4, 'Exactly 4 winning 
cells marked');
        assert(lastMoveRes.data.redWins === preWinRedScore + 1, 'Red wins score 
incremented');
    
        // Verify archived
        const checkArch = await req('/api/archive', { token: averyToken });
        assert(checkArch.data.totalCount === 1, 'Archived 1 completed match');
    
        // Undo the winning move!
        const undoWinRes = await req('/api/game/undo', {
          method: 'POST',
          token: averyToken,
          body: { expectedRevision: averyRev, operationId: 'undo-win-op' }
        });
        assert(undoWinRes.status === 200 && undoWinRes.data.status === 'active',
'Undoing win returns game to active status');
        assert(undoWinRes.data.redWins === preWinRedScore, 'Score increment 
reversed upon undo');
        averyRev = undoWinRes.data.revision;
    
        const checkArchAfterUndo = await req('/api/archive', { token: averyToken
});
        assert(checkArchAfterUndo.data.totalCount === 0, 'Undoing win removes 
match from archive');
    
        // Redo the winning move!
        const redoWinRes = await req('/api/game/redo', {
          method: 'POST',
          token: averyToken,
          body: { expectedRevision: averyRev, operationId: 'redo-win-op' }
        });
        assert(redoWinRes.status === 200 && redoWinRes.data.status === 'Red 
wins', 'Redoing win restores Red wins status');
        assert(redoWinRes.data.redWins === preWinRedScore + 1, 'Redoing win 
restores score increment');
        averyRev = redoWinRes.data.revision;
    
        const checkArchAfterRedo = await req('/api/archive', { token: averyToken
});
        assert(checkArchAfterRedo.data.totalCount === 1, 'Redoing win restores 
match to archive');
    
        // Terminal lock check: move after win should be rejected
        const moveAfterWin = await req('/api/game/move', {
          method: 'POST',
          token: averyToken,
          body: { column: 3, expectedRevision: averyRev, operationId: 
'move-after-win' }
        });
        assert(moveAfterWin.status === 400, 'Move rejected after game is 
finished (terminal locking)');
    
        // -------------------------------------------------------------
        // Test 6: Branching Analyses & Variation Tree
        // -------------------------------------------------------------
        const jArch = await req('/api/archive', { token: tokenJordan });
        const jMatchId = jArch.data.matches[0].matchId;
    
        // Create analysis from step 4
        const anaCreate = await req('/api/analyses', {
          method: 'POST',
          token: tokenJordan,
          body: {
            matchId: jMatchId,
            step: 4,
            name: ' Jordan Study #1  ', // Test trimming whitespace
            operationId: 'ana-create-1'
          }
        });
        assert(anaCreate.status === 201 && anaCreate.data.name === 'Jordan Study
#1', 'Analysis created with trimmed name');
        const anaId = anaCreate.data.id;
        let anaRev = anaCreate.data.revision;
        assert(anaRev === 0, 'New analysis revision starts at 0');
        assert(anaCreate.data.fullSelectedHistory.length === 4, 'Root has 4 
source prefix moves');
    
        // Practice move col 4
        const anaMove1 = await req(`/api/analyses/${anaId}/move`, {
          method: 'POST',
          token: tokenJordan,
          body: { column: 4, expectedRevision: anaRev, operationId: 'ana-m-1' }
        });
        assert(anaMove1.status === 200 && anaMove1.data.revision === 1, 
'Analysis move 1 accepted, revision is 1');
        anaRev = anaMove1.data.revision;
        const node1 = anaMove1.data.selectedNode.id;
    
        // Practice move col 3
        const anaMove2 = await req(`/api/analyses/${anaId}/move`, {
          method: 'POST',
          token: tokenJordan,
          body: { column: 3, expectedRevision: anaRev, operationId: 'ana-m-2' }
        });
        assert(anaMove2.status === 200 && anaMove2.data.revision === 2, 
'Analysis move 2 accepted, revision is 2');
        anaRev = anaMove2.data.revision;
        const node2 = anaMove2.data.selectedNode.id;
    
        // Undo from node2 back to node1
        const anaUndo1 = await req(`/api/analyses/${anaId}/undo`, {
          method: 'POST',
          token: tokenJordan,
          body: { expectedRevision: anaRev, operationId: 'ana-u-1' }
        });
        assert(anaUndo1.status === 200 && anaUndo1.data.selectedNode.id === 
node1, 'Analysis undo selects node1');
        anaRev = anaUndo1.data.revision;
    
        // Branch: play col 5 from node1
        const anaMove3 = await req(`/api/analyses/${anaId}/move`, {
          method: 'POST',
          token: tokenJordan,
          body: { column: 5, expectedRevision: anaRev, operationId: 'ana-m-3' }
        });
        assert(anaMove3.status === 200, 'Analysis branch move col 5 accepted');
        anaRev = anaMove3.data.revision;
        const node3 = anaMove3.data.selectedNode.id;
    
        // Check tree contains both node2 (col 3) and node3 (col 5)
        assert(anaMove3.data.treeNodes.length === 4, 'Tree contains root + 3 
branch nodes (keeping both alternatives)');
    
        // Undo back to node1
        const anaUndo2 = await req(`/api/analyses/${anaId}/undo`, {
          method: 'POST',
          token: tokenJordan,
          body: { expectedRevision: anaRev, operationId: 'ana-u-2' }
        });
        anaRev = anaUndo2.data.revision;
    
        // Replay same column (col 3): should reopen existing node2, NOT 
duplicate!
        const anaReopen = await req(`/api/analyses/${anaId}/move`, {
          method: 'POST',
          token: tokenJordan,
          body: { column: 3, expectedRevision: anaRev, operationId: 
'ana-m-reopen' }
        });
        assert(anaReopen.status === 200 && anaReopen.data.selectedNode.id === 
node2, 'Replaying same column reopens existing child');
        assert(anaReopen.data.treeNodes.length === 4, 'Tree node count did not 
duplicate');
        anaRev = anaReopen.data.revision;
    
        // -------------------------------------------------------------
        // Test 7: Position Comparison
        // -------------------------------------------------------------
        const compRes = await req(`/api/analyses/${anaId}/compare`, {
          method: 'POST',
          token: tokenJordan,
          body: { nodeAId: node2, nodeBId: node3 }
        });
        assert(compRes.status === 200, 'Position comparison succeeds');
        assert(compRes.data.sharedOpeningMovesCount === 5, 'Shared opening moves
count is 5 (4 prefix + 1 shared col 4 move)');
        assert(compRes.data.differingCells.length === 2, 'Differing cells count 
is 2');
    
        // -------------------------------------------------------------
        // Test 8: Bounded Tactical Reports (Minimax Proof Tree)
        // -------------------------------------------------------------
        // Test invalid depths
        const badDepth1 = await 
req(`/api/analyses/${anaId}/tactical?nodeId=${node1}&depth=0`, { token: 
tokenJordan });
        assert(badDepth1.status === 400, 'Depth 0 rejected with 400');
        const badDepth2 = await 
req(`/api/analyses/${anaId}/tactical?nodeId=${node1}&depth=5`, { token: 
tokenJordan });
        assert(badDepth2.status === 400, 'Depth 5 rejected with 400');
        const badDepth3 = await 
req(`/api/analyses/${anaId}/tactical?nodeId=${node1}&depth=2.5`, { token: 
tokenJordan });
        assert(badDepth3.status === 400, 'Fractional depth rejected with 400');
    
        // Valid tactical search at depth 2
        const tacRes = await 
req(`/api/analyses/${anaId}/tactical?nodeId=${node1}&depth=2`, { token: 
tokenJordan });
        assert(tacRes.status === 200, 'Tactical search at depth 2 succeeds');
        assert(tacRes.data.columns.length === 7, 'Returns evaluations for all 7 
columns');
        assert(tacRes.data.tree && tacRes.data.tree.id === 'root', 'Returns full
explanation proof tree');
    
        // -------------------------------------------------------------
        // Test 9: Branch Transplant Preview & Commit
        // -------------------------------------------------------------
        // Cannot transplant from root
        const badTrans = await req(`/api/analyses/${anaId}/transplant/preview`, 
{
          method: 'POST',
          token: tokenJordan,
          body: {
            sourceNodeId: anaCreate.data.rootNodeId,
            destAnalysisId: anaId,
            destNodeId: node3
          }
        });
        assert(badTrans.status === 400, 'Transplant from root rejected with 
400');
    
        // Valid preview transplant of node1 (which has children node2 and 
node3) onto node3
        const transPrev = await req(`/api/analyses/${anaId}/transplant/preview`,
{
          method: 'POST',
          token: tokenJordan,
          body: {
            sourceNodeId: node1,
            destAnalysisId: anaId,
            destNodeId: node3
          }
        });
        assert(transPrev.status === 200 && transPrev.data.previewId, 'Transplant
preview succeeds');
        assert(transPrev.data.previewNodes.length === 3, 'Preview contains node1
and its 2 children');
    
        // Commit transplant
        const transCommit = await 
req(`/api/analyses/${anaId}/transplant/commit`, {
          method: 'POST',
          token: tokenJordan,
          body: {
            previewId: transPrev.data.previewId,
            operationId: 'trans-commit-op-1'
          }
        });
        assert(transCommit.status === 200 && transCommit.data.success, 
'Transplant commit succeeds');
        assert(Object.keys(transCommit.data.nodeMapping).length === 3, 'Node 
mapping returned for all copied nodes');
    
        // Second commit of already committed preview must be rejected
        const dupCommit = await req(`/api/analyses/${anaId}/transplant/commit`, 
{
          method: 'POST',
          token: tokenJordan,
          body: {
            previewId: transPrev.data.previewId,
            operationId: 'trans-commit-op-2'
          }
        });
        assert(dupCommit.status === 400, 'Re-committing already committed 
preview rejected with 400');
    
        console.log(`\n🎉 ALL ${testsPassed} ASSERTIONS PASSED WITH 0 
FAILURES!`);
      } catch (err) {
        console.error('Fatal test error:', err);
      } finally {
        server.close();
        process.exit(testsFailed > 0 ? 1 : 0);
      }
    });

const { app } = require('./server');

html.text()).length);

    console.error(e);
  } finally {
    server.close();
  }
});

Reasoning:
Okay, here's my take on those thoughts, framed as my own:

Right, everything's checked off. All the ducks are in a row, every requirement 
is met, and I've verified the implementation from top to bottom. No loose ends,

Finish with message:
DropLine is fully built, tested, and ready.

Key Features & Implementations:
1. **Authentication & Session Isolation**:
   - Seed accounts imported from `/assets/artifacts/dropline_seed.xlsx` 
(`avery@dropline.test`, `jordan@dropline.test`).
   - Unpredictable Bearer tokens stored in SQLite (`dropline.db`).
   - Account-wide sign-out revocation across sessions.
   - Account state isolation: board, scores, history, archives, and analyses 
stay isolated per account.

2. **Competitive Gameplay & Mechanics**:
   - 7-column by 6-row (42 cells) Connect Four board with gravity, alternating 
turns, full-column rejection (`Column N is full`), and terminal locking.
   - 4-in-a-row detection in horizontal, vertical, and both diagonal directions 
with winning cell markers.
   - Server-backed undo and redo: undoing a win/draw reverses score increment 
and removes the round from archive; redoing restores the exact result and score.
   - Idempotency receipts and integer revision checks returning 409 Conflict 
with `"Game updated in another tab"` when stale.

3. **Completed Match Archive & Read-only Replay**:
   - Total record count and latest 10 completed matches (newest first).
   - Replay stepping with range slider, Previous/Next controls, and 42 
accessible grid cells.

4. **Branching Analysis Workspace**:
   - Create named, frozen analyses from any replay position (step 0 through 
final move).
   - Multi-branching variation tree preserving all continuations on undo + 
alternate moves.
   - Ambiguous redo resolution with branch continuation selection.
   - Replaying the same column from the same parent reopens the existing child 
node.
   - Two-position comparison showing shared opening move count and differing 
cells with visible non-color markers and coordinates.

5. **Study Tools: Branch Transplant & Bounded Tactical Reports**:
   - Branch preview and atomic commit onto any destination position, evaluating 
fresh gravity, turns, wins/draws, and returning node mappings while reporting 
deterministic illegal move paths.
   - Bounded minimax tactical search (1–4 plies) from player-to-move's 
perspective evaluating forced win, forced loss (best resistance), forced draw, 
or not established, with full browsable proof trees and 42-cell board 
inspectors.

6. **Accessibility & Responsive Interface**:
   - Exactly 42 grid cells with descriptive accessible names naming row, column,
occupant, and winning state.
   - Column controls supporting Left/Right/Home/End/Enter/Space keyboard 
navigation.
   - Mobile responsive layout down to 375px width without horizontal overflow.
   - Support for `prefers-reduced-motion`.

7. **Documentation**:
   - `APP_MANIFEST.md` created in `/app` detailing all API routes, database path
(`SQLite path: /app/dropline.db`), and startup command (`node /app/server.js`).
